4. innūṟpāviṟ kūṟappaṭum tuṟikaḷ cila puṟattōṉukku uriyaṉa vākavum, cila akattōṉukku uriyaṉavākavum āciriyar tolkāppiya ṉārāl varintu kūṟappaṭutalāl, innūṟpāviṟ kūṟappaṭum tuṟikaḷ yāvum puṟattōṉ  akattōṉ ākiya irutiṟavēntarkkum oṉṟāyc ceṉṟu uriyaṉa eṉakkūṟutal tolkāppiyaṉār karuttukku ēṟpuṭiya tākātu. innūṟpāviṟ puṟattōṉukku uriyavākac collappaṭṭa tuṟikaḷ akattō ṉukkum, akattōṉukku uriyavākac collappaṭṭa tuṟikaḷ puṟattōṉukkum oppavuriyaṉavāka vaḻaṅkiya piṟkāla marapiṉi aṭiyoṟṟit tōṉṟiyatu perumporuḷ viḷakkam eṉṉum nūlākum. itu nacciṉārkkiṉiyar uriyil maṭṭum eṭutturikkap paṭukiṟatu. piṟkāla nūlākiya itaṉkaṇ aminta tuṟikaḷ ellāvaṟṟiyum innūṟkup paṉṉū ṟāṇṭukaḷ muṟpaṭṭut tōṉṟiya tolkāppiyattil  aṭakkutal ilakkiyaṅ kaṇṭataṟku  ilakkaṇam iyampal eṉṉum nacciṉārkkiṉiyaratu ārvattiṉ pāṟpaṭumēyaṉṟi varalāṟṟu muṟikku ēṟpuṭiyataṉṟām eṉat terital vēṇṭum.
uḷḷuṟi uvamam
a.
1.	uḷḷuṟi uvamam ēṉi uvamameṉat
	taḷḷā tākum tiṇiyuṇar vakiyē	(to.po.49)
2.	uḷḷuṟi teyvam oḻintati nilameṉak
	koḷḷum eṉpa kuṟiyaṟin tōrē.	(to.po.50)
3.	uḷḷuṟut titaṉō ṭottup poruḷmuṭikeṉa
	uḷḷuṟut turippatē uḷḷuṟi uvamam	              (to.po.51)
4.	piṟitoṭu paṭātu piṟappoṭu nōkki
	muṉṉa marapiṟ kūṟuṅ kālit
	tuṇivoṭu varūum tuṇiviṉōr koḷiṉē  	            (to.po.294)
ā.
	avaṟṟuḷ,
	uḷḷuṟi uvamam uyttuṇar vakittāyp
	puḷḷoṭum vilaṅkoṭum piṟavoṭum pulappaṭum	(na.a.238)
i.
iḷam:
1.	(uvamavakiyāṉ aintiṇikkum uriyatōr iyal puṇarttutal).
	uḷḷuṟikkaṇvarum uvamamum oḻinta uvamamum eṉa iruvaki yālum tiṇiyuṇarvaki tappātu ākum.
2.	(uḷḷuṟiyāmāṟu uṇarttutal)
	uḷḷuṟiyāvatu  karupporuṭṭeyvam oḻinta poruḷi  iṭa mākak koṇṭuvarum eṉṟu colluvar ilakkaṇam aṟintōr. kuṟi  ilakkaṇam.
3.	(uḷḷuṟiyuvamam āmāṟu uṇarttutal)
	uḷḷuṟuttappaṭṭa  karupporuḷi  uḷḷuṟuttukkarutiya poruḷ itaṉōṭu ottup poruḷmuṭika eṉa uḷḷuṟuttuk kūṟuvatē uḷḷuṟi uvamam. 
	eṉavē uvamiyāṟ koḷḷum viṉi payaṉ mey uru aṉṟip poruḷuvamiyāṟ koḷḷappaṭuvatu.
nac:
1.	(uvamaviyaluḷ akattiṇik kikōḷ iraṇṭaṟkum potuvaki yāṉ uriyatoṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu).
	mēṟkūṟum  uḷḷuṟi uvamamtāṉ ēṉiya uvamameṉṟu kūṟumpaṭi uvamiyum uvamikkappaṭum poruḷumāy niṉṟatu. akattiṇi uṇartaṟkuk  karuviyākiya uḷḷuṟi  uvamampōla ellāt tiṇiyiyum uṇaruṅkūṟṟit taḷḷātāy varum nallicippulavar ceyyuṭ ceyyiṉ eṉṟavāṟu.
	eṉavē ēṉiyōr ceyyiṉ ṟāṉuṇarum vakittāy niṟkum eṉṟavāṟām.
	iṉit taḷḷātu eṉṟataṉāṉē... ēṉi uvamamāy niṉṟu karupporu ḷoṭu kūṭic ciṟappiyātu tāṉē tiṇip poruḷ tōṉṟuvittu niṟpaṉa  pōlavum, karupporuḷ tāṉē uvamamāy niṉṟu uḷḷuṟip poruḷ taruvaṉavum piṟavum vēṟupaṭa varuvaṉavum itaṉāṉ amikka. itu puṟattiṟkum potu. itaṉāṉ uḷḷuṟi uvamamum ēṉiyuvamamumeṉa uvamam iraṇṭē yeṉpatu kūṟiṉār.
2.	(itumuṟiyē uḷḷuṟi uvamaṅkūṟukiṉṟatu).
	uḷḷuṟi eṉappaṭṭa uvamam teyvam mutaliya karupporuḷuṭ ṭeyvatti  oḻittu oḻinta karupporuḷkaḷē taṉakkut tōṉṟum nilaṉākak koṇṭu pulappaṭumeṉṟu kūṟupa ilakkaṇam aṟintōr eṉṟavāṟu.
	eṉavē uṇavu mutaliya paṟṟiya apporuḷ nikaḻcci piṟitoṉṟaṟku uvamiyākac ceytal uḷḷuṟi uvamamāyiṟṟu.
	iṉi, aḵtu uḷḷattāṉ uyttuṇara vēṇṭumeṉa mēṟkūṟukiṉṟār.
3.	(ituvum aṅṅaṉam piṟanta uḷḷuṟiyuvamattiṉip poruṭku upakāram paṭa uvamam koḷḷumāṟu kūṟu kiṉṟatu).
	tāṉpulappaṭak kūṟukiṉṟa ivvuvamattōṭē pulappaṭak kūṟāta uvamikkappaṭum poruḷ ottu muṭivatāka eṉṟu pulavaṉ taṉ uḷḷattē karutit tāṉ aṅṅaṉaṅ karutum māttiriyē aṉṟiyum  kēṭṭōr maṉattiṉ kaṇṇum avvāṟē nikaḻttuvittu aṅṅaṉam uṇarttuvataṟku uṟuppākiya collellām niṟiyak koṇṭu muṭivatu uḷḷuṟi uvamam eṉṟavāṟu.
	itaṉāṉē pulavaṉ tāṉ karutiyatu kūṟātavaḻiyum kēṭṭōr ivaṉ karutiya poruḷ īteṉṟārāyntu kōṭaṟkuk karuviyākiya cila coṟkiṭappac ceytal vēṇṭumeṉpatu karuttāyiṟṟu.
ī.
nāvalar:
1. (akattuṟic ceyyuṭkaḷil varum uripporuṭ pakutikaḷ ellāvaṟ ṟiṟkum ciṟappāka urittām uvamavaki kūṟukiṟatu).
	aintiṇiyuṇarttum uripporuṭ pakutikaḷil uḷḷuṟi uvama māṉatu maṟṟiya uvamattōṟṟampōla arukāmal vantupayilum.
	īṟṟēkāram pirinili, akattuṟikaḷuḷ tiṇiyuṇar vakiyi vēṟu  pirittaliṉ avvakiyallāp piṟa akappakutikaḷil ēṉiyuvamam arukātu eṉpatu kuṟippu.
	uvamam, veḷippaṭat tōṉṟum uvamamum, uḷḷuṟi uvamamum eṉa iruvakittām. ivaṟṟuḷ uḷḷuṟi uvamamē tiṇiyuṇarum uripporuṭ pakutikaḷic ciṟappit taṟku urittākum. ēṉiya  uvamam avvāṟu akavoḻukka vakikaḷukkuc ciṟavātākiyāl, akattuṟikaḷil tiṇiyuṇarum pakutikaḷukku attuṇiyā āṭcipeṟutal illi. 'taḷḷātākum' eṉṟataṉāl ēṉi uvamam arukippayilum eṉpatum, uḷḷuṟi uvamam avvāṟaṉṟip peruvaraviṟṟām eṉpatum peṟappaṭum.
	uḷḷuṟi, uvamattiṉ oru pakutiyāy aṭaṅkumēṉum puṟattiṟkē peritum urimikoḷḷum ēṉi uvamam pōlātu akattiṟkē ciṟanturiyatu ātaliṉ itu uvama viyaliṟ kūṟappeṟātu akavoḻuk kam kūṟum ittiṇiyiṟ kūṟappaṭṭatu. ēṉi uvamatti akat tiṇikku  urittallātatu eṉa ēṉi uvamac cūttirattiṉ kīḻ virivuriyil nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟuvar. cāṉṟōr ceyyuṭkaḷil arukiya āṭci akattiṇiyilum ēṉi uvamam peṟutaliṉ, ataṉi aṟavē akattiṇik kurittilli eṉa vilakkutal poruntātu. iṉṉum iḷampūraṇar uḷḷuṟi uvamam oḻinta uvamam eṉa iruvakiyālum, 'tiṇiyuṇarvaki tappātākum' eṉak kūṟuva tālum ēṉi uvamattiṟku arukiyēṉum akat tiṇikkaṇ āṭciyuṇmi teḷiyappaṭum.
2.	(uḷḷuṟi uvamattiṟku nilikkaḷam uṇarttukiṟatu).
	akattiṇiyiṟ payilum uḷḷuṟi uvamam karup poruḷkaḷuḷ teyvam nīkki maṟṟiyavaṟṟit taṉakku nilikkaḷa ṉākat taḻuvi varum eṉṟu kūṟuvar oppiyalaṟintōr.
	oppiyal  aṟintōr eṉṉum eḻuvāy kūṟuvar eṉṉum viṉikku ēṟpa avāy niliyāṟ peṟappaṭṭatu.
3.	(uḷḷuṟi uvamattiṉ iyalpu kūṟukiṉṟatu).
	veḷippaṭakkūṟum poruḷōṭu uḷḷum poruḷum ottu muṭiyu māṟu uḷḷattu ūṉṟi nuṇuki uṇara amintu muṭivatu uḷḷuṟi uvamamākum.
	iṟuvati eṉpatu, ēṟṟi eṉpatupōla aiyīṟu peṟṟu muṭinta peyar. (kaṉakacapāpatippiḷḷi: pāṭam: 1) uḷḷuṟut turippati 
2. uḷḷuṟuttiṟuvati.
mu.a:
1.	uḷḷuṟiyuvamamāvatu, uvamam oṉṟē veḷippaṭak kūṟappaṭṭu aḵtu uvamikkappaṭum poruḷ mutaliyavaṟṟik kuṟippāl uṇartti niṟpatu.
	ēṉiyuvamamāvatu, uvamam, uvamikkappaṭum poruḷ, potut taṉmi, uvamurupu eṉṉum nāṉkum veḷippaṭṭu niṉṟu poruḷi iṉitu viḷakkuvatu.
	akattiṇikkē uriyatāyum kaḷavu kaṟpeṉṉum iraṇṭaṟkum  potuvāṉatāyum varum uḷḷuṟi yuvamattip potu vilakkaṇam kūṟum ivviyaliṟ kūṟuvār ēṉiyuvamamum uṭaṉ kūṟiṉār.
	'itu puṟattiṟkum potu' eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyar eḻutiyuḷḷār. ivar, akattiṇik kikōḷ iraṇṭaṟkum potuvāy varuvatu iv vuḷḷuṟi uvamameṉa innūṟpāviṉ kīḻk karutturi eḻuti yiruttalāṉum, iṉpat tuṟi paṟṟiya ceytikaḷaṉṟip puṟapporuṭ ceytikaḷ maṟittuk kūṟa vēṇṭāmi yāṉum orōvaḻi maṟittuk kūṟappaṭumēl atu piṟitu moḻitaleṉṉum aṇiyiṉ pāṟpaṭu maṉṟi uḷḷuṟi uvamam eṉappaṭāmiyāṉum ''aṟumarup peḻiṟ kali pulippāṟpaṭṭeṉa" eṉṉum puṟappāṭṭiṉ (23)  pakutikkuk kali pulippāṟ paṭṭeṉac ''ciṟumaṟi taḻīiya maṭappiṇi, paṟantali vēḷiveṇpūk kaṟikkum" eṉpatu avaṉ pakivarik koṉṟavaḻi avar peṇṭir tam iḷamputalvari yōmpaṟ poruṭṭu iṟantu paṭātu aṭaku tiṉṟu uyirvāḻkiṉṟār eṉpatōr poruḷ tōṉṟa niṉṟatu eṉa appuṟanāṉūṟṟu uriyāciriyar eḻutiṉāraṉṟi ataṉi uḷḷuṟi uvamameṉṟu kūṟāmi yāṉum uṭaṉuṟiyuvamam mutaliya uḷḷuṟiyintum akat tiṇikkaṇ varumaṉṟip puṟattiṇikkaṇ vārāmi yāṉum itu puṟattiṟkum potuveṉa eḻutiyiruppatu ārāyattakkatu.
	itu, kaḷaviṟkum kaṟpiṟkum potuveṉṟēṉum, ēṉi yuvamam puṟattiṟkum potuveṉṟēṉum avar eḻuti yiruntavuri, puṟattiṟ kum potuveṉap piḻipaṭṭirukkalāmō eṉa eṇṇutaṟkum iṭamirukkiṉṟatu. 
2.	uvamam paṟṟiya uḷḷuṟi teyvam oḻinta karupporuḷi yiṭamākak koṇṭu tōṉṟumeṉa itaṉāṟ kūṟiyuḷḷār. eṉavē, uṭaṉuṟi mutaliya nāṉku uḷḷuṟikaḷum karupporuḷiṭamākat tōṉṟā eṉpatu āciriyar karuttu. iṅṅaṉam  koḷḷāmal uṭaṉuṟiyākiya iṟicci eṉṟa uḷḷuṟiyum karupporuḷ iṭamākat tōṉṟu meṉak koṇṭu viḷakkam kūṟuvatu kaṟpavarkku mayakkatti viḷippatākum.
3.	ceyyuṭ ceyyum pulavaṉ yāṉ veḷippaṭak kūṟukiṉṟa ivvuvamat tōṭottu aṅṅaṉam veḷippaṭak kūṟappaṭāta uvamikkappaṭum poruḷ muṭivatāka eṉṟu, taṉ uḷḷattilē amittukkoṇṭu ataṟkēṟpa vēṇṭuñ coṟka ḷellām acceyyuḷiṉkaṇṇē niṟiyap peyturip patē uḷḷuṟi uvamam eṉṟavāṟu. ivvāṟu poruḷ koḷḷutal eḷitu. muṭikeṉa  eṉpuḻi viyaṅkōḷīṟu tokkuniṉṟatu.


uḷḷuṟi vaki
a.
1.	uṭaṉuṟi uvamam cuṭṭunaki ciṟappeṉak
	keṭalaru marapiṉ uḷḷuṟi aintē	(to.po.238)
2.	antamil ciṟappiṉ ākiya iṉpam	      
	taṉvayiṉ varutalum vakutta paṇpē	(to.po.239)
3.	maṅkala moḻiyum aviyal moḻiyum
	māṟil āṇmiyiṟ colliya moḻiyum
	kūṟiya maruṅkiṟ koḷḷum eṉpa.	(to.po.240)
i.
iḷam:
1.	(uḷḷuṟiyāmāṟu uṇarttutal).
	uṭaṉuṟiyum uvamamum cuṭṭum nakiyum ciṟappumeṉak keṭalaru marapiṉiyuṭiya uḷḷuṟi aintu vakippaṭum eṉṟavāṟu.
	uḷḷuṟiyāvatu piṟitoru poruḷ pulappaṭumāṟu niṟpatoṉṟu.  atu karupporuḷ paṟṟivarum eṉpatu akattiṇiyiyaluḷ  (50) kūṟappaṭṭatu.
	uṭaṉuṟiyāvatu, uṭaṉuṟiva toṉṟic colla ataṉāṉē piṟitoru poruḷ viḷaṅkuvatu.
	uvamam eṉpatu, uvamiyic colla uvamikkappaṭum poruḷ tōṉṟuvatu.
	cuṭṭu eṉpatu, oruporuḷic cuṭṭip piṟitōr poruṭ paṭutal.
	nakiyāvatu, nakiyiṉāṟ piṟitoru poruḷuṇara niṟṟal.
	ciṟappu eṉpatu, itaṟkuc ciṟantatu iḵtu eṉak kūṟuvataṉāṉē piṟitōr poruḷkoḷak kiṭappatu.
2.	(ituvum uḷḷuṟip pāṟpaṭuvatōr poruḷ uṇarttutal)
	'antamilāta ciṟappiṉākiya iṉpattiṭattu uḷḷuṟip poruṇmi varutalum vakutta iyalpu eṉṟavāṟu.
	'antamil ciṟappu' eṉpatu mēṉmēluñ ciṟappuc ceytal.
3.	(ituvum uḷḷuṟip pāṟpaṭuvatōr col uṇarttutal).
	maṅkala moḻi mutalākac collappaṭṭaṉavum uḷḷuṟip pāṟpaṭum eṉṟavāṟu.
	maṅkalamoḻiyāvatu, maṅkalattāṟ kūṟuñcol. atu cettārit tuñciṉār eṉṟal.
	aviyal moḻiyāvatu, iṭakkaraṭakkik kūṟutal. atu kaṇkaḻīi varutum eṉṟal.
	māṟilāṇmiyiṟ colliya moḻiyāvatu, oruvaṉic ciṅkam vantatu eṉṟāṟpōl kūṟuvatu.
	aviyellām collāṟ poruḷpaṭāmiyiṉ uḷḷuṟip pāṟpaṭum.
nac:
1.	(mēlveḷippaṭak kiḷappaṉa kūṟippiṉ veḷippaṭāmaṟ kiḷakkum uḷḷuṟi iṉitteṉkiṉṟatu).
	nāṉku nilattum uḷavāy annilattuṭaṉuṟiyum karupporuḷāṟ piṟitoṉṟu payappamaṟittuk kūṟum iṟicciyum, akkaruvāṟ koḷḷum uḷḷuṟi yuvamamum, ēṉiyuvamamum, uṭaṉuṟi yuvamamum aṉṟi nakiyum ciṟappum  paṟṟātu vāḷātu oṉṟu niṉintu oṉṟu colvaṉavum aṉpuṟu takuna iṟicciyuṭ cuṭṭi varuvaṉavum, nakiyāṭi oṉṟu niṉittu oṉṟu kūṟutalum, ēṉiyuvamam niṉṟu uḷḷuṟiyuvamattit taruṅkarup poruṭkuc ciṟappuk koṭuttu niṟṟalum eṉṟu keṭutalilātākiya muṟimiyiṉi yuṭiya uḷḷuṟi aintu vakippaṭum.
	oṉṟaṉi uḷḷuṟuttu ataṉi veḷippaṭāmaṟ kūṟaliṉ avaṟṟi uḷḷuṟiyām eṉṟār.
	iṟiccitāṉē (229) iṟicciyiṟ piṟakkum (230) eṉpaṉavaṟṟuḷ iṟiccikkutāraṇam kāṭṭiṉām.  uvamam uvama iyaluṭ kāṭṭutum. aṉpuṟu takuna eṉpataṉuḷ (231) ēṉiyataṟkutāraṇam kāṭṭiṉām.
	(kāraṇam eṉpatu utāraṇam eṉṟiruttal vēṇṭum. ka.ve.).
	uḷḷuṟi yuvamam ēṉi uvamam eṉṉuñ cūttirattu virikatir  maṇṭilam' eṉṉum marutakkaliyuṭ ciṟappuk koṭuttu niṉṟatu kāṭṭiṉām.
	aṟattoṭu niliyum poḻutum āṟum mutaliyaṉavum cevvaṉam kūṟappaṭutaliṉ ivi karantē kūṟappaṭutaliṟ 'keṭalarumarapiṉ' eṉṟār. ivi tōḻikkum talivikkum uriyavāṟu ceyyuṭkaḷi nōkkiyuṇarka.
2.	(muṟkūṟiya uḷḷuṟi paṟṟit talivaṟku varuvatōr vaḻuvamik kiṉṟatu).
	muṟkūṟiya uḷḷuṟi aintāṉum avarkaḷ uṇṭākkiya muṭivilāta ciṟappiṉiyuṭiya iṉpam talivaṉ kaṇṇum nikaḻntu iṉpañ ceytalum kāmattukku mutalāciriyaṉ vakutta ilakkaṇam.
	talivaṉ taṉmi eṉpatoṉṟiṉṟi tantaṉmiyeṉak karututaliṉ yām oṉṟi niṉintu oṉṟu kūṟiṉum avaṉ muṉiyātu iṉpa meṉak koḷvaṉeṉak kūṟiyavaṟṟi avaṉ ivi iṉpan tarumeṉṟu kōṭaliṉ vaḻuvamikkap paṭṭaṉa.
3.	(mēṉmūṉṟu poruḷum vaḻuvāyamika eṉṟaliṉ eytātatu eytuvittatu). paṭam  viciya moḻiyum.
	talivaṟkut tīṅku varumeṉṟu uṭkoṇṭu tōḻiyum taliviyum ataṟku añci avaṉi vaḻuttutalum, talivaṉ tammi vañcit tāṉākat taliviyum tōḻiyum kūṟalum, māṟupāṭillāta āḷun taṉmiyiṭattē paḻipaṭak kūṟiya moḻiyum vaḻuvamitiyākak kūṟiya ilakkaṇat tiṭattē koḷḷum moḻiyeṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar.
	viiyamoḻi  tīṅkivitta moḻiyumām.
ī.
ka.ve:
iṅkuk kūṟappaṭṭa uḷḷuṟi aintaṉuḷ uṭaṉuṟi eṉpatu iṟicci yiyum, uvamam eṉpatu uḷḷuṟi uvamattiṉiyum, cuṭṭu eṉpatu aṉpuṟutakuna iṟicciyuṭ cuṭṭutaliyum kuṟittaṉa eṉak koḷḷutalē poruttamuṭiyatākum. nakik kuṟipput tōṉṟa oṉṟu niṉintu oṉṟu kūṟutal naki eṉṉum uḷḷuṟiyākum eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyar tarum viḷakkam ēṟpuṭiyatēyākum.
uṭaṉuṟikkum nakikkum iḷampūraṇar tarum viḷakkamum eṭuttuk kāṭṭum kuṟitta avvan nikaḻccikku maṭṭumaṉṟi ēṉiyavaṟṟukkuc cellāmiyiṉ avaṟṟi uḷḷuṟik kuriya vakiyākak koḷḷu taṟku iyalavilli. iḷampūraṇar cuṭṭeṉṉum uḷḷuṟikku eṭuttuk kāṭṭiya 'toṭinōkki' eṉavarum tirukkuṟaḷ (1279) kuṟippaṟi vuṟuttal eṉṟa talippil talimakaḷ kuṟippiṉi veḷiyiṭṭurippatākaliṉ ataṉi maṟittukkūṟum uḷḷuṟi vakiyākak koḷḷutaṟ kiyalavilli.


uṟaḻkali
a.
kūṟṟum māṟṟamum iṭiyiṭi miṭintum
pōkkiṉ ṟākal uṟaḻkalik kiyalpē	            (to.po.458)
i.
iḷam:
(uṟaḻkaliyāmāṟu uṇarttutal)
uṟaḻkalip pāviṟku ilakkaṇam, kūṟṟum māṟṟamum viravi vantu curitakamiṉṟi muṭital eṉṟavāṟu.
itaṉik koccakak kaliyiṉpiṉ vittamiyāṉ ak koccaka vuṟuppiṉ oppaṉa itaṟku uṟuppākak koḷḷappaṭum.
(eṭu.)
yārivaṉ eṅkūntal.	(kali. 89)
ippāṭṭuc curitakamiṉṟi vantavāṟu kaṇṭu koḷka.
eṟṟiṟku? iṟutiyiṉkaṇ vantatu curitakam ākātō eṉiṉ, curitakamākātu. curitakamāvatu ātippāṭṭiṉum iṭi nilippāṭṭiṉum uḷḷa poruḷit tokuttu muṭippatu. iḵtu aṉṉataṉṟu eṉka.
pērā:
(muṟiyāṉē uṟaḻkaliyuṇarttutal).
oruvar oṉṟu kūṟutaṟku maṟumāṟṟam maṟṟoruvar kūṟic ceṉṟu piṉṉar niṉṟatu uṟaḻkali.
'pōkkiṉṟu' eṉavē taravu peṟutalum pāṭṭiṭi miṭitalum,  aiñcīraṭukkalum, āṟumeypeṟutalum, oḻinta coṟcīraṭi peṟuta lum pāmayaṅki varutalum, ampō taraṅkattiṟkōtiya aḷavi peṟutalum ellām veṇkalip pāṭṭiṟkup pōlamēṉiṉṟa atikārattāṟ peṟappaṭuva tāyiṟṟu.
'pōkkiṉṟākal iyalpu'  eṉavē iyalpiṉṟi vikāra vakiyāl cila pōkkuṭiyavumām eṉṟāṉām. atuvum 'palporuṭkēṟpiṉ nallatu kōṭal' eṉpataṉāṉ veḷḷic curitakam oḻittu  āciriyac curitakamē koḷḷappaṭum. eṉṉi? veḷḷic curitakam pala vanta vaḻi oṉṟaṉic curitakameṉalākāmiyāṉum, accuritakam iraṇṭum 'pōkkiyal vakiyē vip peṉappaṭumē' eṉak kūṟappaṭṭa ilakkaṇatta alla vāyiṉum, 'eḻucīr iṟuti āciriyaṅkali yeṉa' niṉṟavaḻi appāṭṭu muṭintu kāṭṭi niṟṟalāṉum atuvē koṇṭām ciṟupāṉmi eṉpatu.
maṟṟut taraviṉṟi varuvaṉavum uḷavāl eṉiṉ, itaṟkut taravu mutalāyiṉa uṟuppu vitantu ōtiyatilaṉākaliṉ  aḵtu ārāycci yiṉṟeṉpatu; aṟṟaṉṟu taravum pōkkum uṭaṉkūṟiyataṉāl 'pōkkum pāṭṭiṭi miṭintum' eṉṉum atikāram paṟṟi īṇṭup pōkku  vilakkiṉami yiṉ atu poruntātu; maṟṟu eṉṉi karutiyataṉiṉ, itaṟku atikāram paṭa niṟīiya kaliveṇpāṭṭu ōtiya cūttirattiṟ pāṭṭiṉi iṭikūṟātu taravum pōkkum uṭaṉ  kūṟiyataṉāṟ pōkkiṉ ilakkaṇattiyum peṟum eṉṟāṉām. ataṉāṉē, īṇṭu vilakkuṇṭa pōkkup pōlac ciṟupāṉmi taraviṉṟiyum varumeṉpatu. ikkaruttiṉāṉ aṉṟē itaṉip pāniliyōṭu kūṟātu taravu vakippaṭum koccakattiṉ piṉṉar vippā ṉāyiṟṟeṉpatu.
iṉippōkkuṭiyaṉa taraviṉṟi vārā, avi ōriṉam eṉṟu ōtap paṭṭamiyiṉ eṉpatu. maṟṟu itaṟkup pōkkiṉmi yum ilakkaṇa mākaliṉ atupaṟṟiyum peyar koḷḷāmō eṉiṉ, pōkkiṉmi ciṟupāṉmi koccakattiṟkum urimiyiṉ avvaki uriyatōr ilakkaṇam paṟṟip peyar kūṟāṉ eṉpatu.
maṟṟu itaṉiyum koccakam eṉṟakkāl iḻukkeṉṉi eṉiṉ, nāṭakac ceyyuṭpōla vēṟuvēṟu tuṇiporuḷa vākiyum pala toṭarntamiyiṟ peritum vēṟupāṭuṭimi nōkkiyum itu poruḷatikāramātalāṟ poruḷ vēṟupāṭu  paṟṟiyum varalāṟṟumuṟimi paṟṟiyum vēṟu ceyyu ḷeṉṟāṉ eṉpatu.
nac:
(pōkkiṉṟātal  pāṭam).
(muṟiyāṉē uṟaḻkali kūṟukiṉṟatu).
oruvar oṉṟu kūṟutaṟku maṟumāṟṟam maṟṟoruvar kūṟic ceṉṟu piṉṉar avaṟṟi yaṭakkuvatōr curitakamiṉṟi muṭivatu uṟaḻkaliyām. 'pōk kiṉṟu'  eṉavē oḻinta uṟuppukkaḷ ellām kaliveṇpāṭṭiṟku ōtiyavāṟē varum eṉpatūum, pāmayaṅki varum eṉpatūum, ampōta raṅ kattiṉ aḷavē aḷavu eṉpatūum mēṉiṉṟa atikārattāṟ peṟṟām.
'iyal'peṉavē iyalpaṉṟi vikāravakiyāṟ cila pōkkuṭiya vātalum avitām āciriyamēyākalum  cila taravum  pōkkumiṉṟi  varutalum ciṟupāṉmi koḷka. pōkkuṭiyaṉa taraviṉṟi vārāmiyum koḷka.
veḷḷik koccakam pala vantu avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟic curitakam eṉiṉ ataṟkup pōkkiyaṟ poruṇmi iṉmiyāṉ ataṉi nīkkip pōkkiyaṟ poruṇmi iṉṟēṉum āciriyam vantuḻi appāṭṭiṟ kōr muṭivu kāṭṭi niṟṟaliṉ āciriyamē pōkkiyalām eṉak koṇṭām.
nāṭakac ceyyuṭpōla vēṟuvēṟu tuṇiporuḷavākiya pala toṭarnta vēṟupāṭu nōkkiyum poruḷvēṟupāṭu  nōkkiyum varalāṟṟu muṟimi nōkkiyum ataṟkuriyatōr ilakkaṇattāl uṟaḻkaliyeṉa vēṟōr ceyyuḷeṉṟār, koccakattil aṭaṅ kāmiyiṉ. ''marapunili tirital ceyyuṭkilli; marapuvaḻippaṭṭa colliṉāṉa" eṉa marapu kūṟaliṉ (to.mara.90).
u.
ā.ku:	
uṟaḻtal  etiriṭṭuk kūṟutal;  tarukkamiṭutal, taṭi viṭikaḷāl viḷakkutal eṉpaṉa uṟaḻvakiyē. kūṟṟum māṟṟamum iṭiyiṭi varutalāl,  kalikkeṉa yāppuṟavip poruḷukku ēṟpa amittuk koṇṭatu ivvuṟaḻkaliyām. poruḷnili karutiyamittuk koḷḷap paṭṭa yāppiyal vaḷarcci vakiyuḷ ivvuṟaḻ kalikkuc cīriya iṭamuṇṭu. piṉṉē eḻunta maṉōṉ maṇiyam pōṉṟa nāṭakak kāppiya muṉṉōṭic cāṉṟu mēlināṭṭu nūlvaki eṉpataṉuṭaṉ, ivvuṟaḻ kalikkum paṅkuṇṭu eṉalām.


uṉṉa nili
a.
ōṭā, uṭalvēn taṭukkiya uṉṉanili	             (to.po.63)
ā.
tuṉṉarun tuppiṉ toṭukaḻal maṉṉaṉi
uṉṉam cērtti uṟupukaḻ malintaṉṟu	(pu.ve.4)
i.
iḷam:
ōṭāta vekuṇṭa vēntaric cārttiya uṉṉanili.
uṉṉam eṉpatu maram. atu taṉ nāṭṭakattuk kēṭu varuṅkāl ulaṟiyum, vārāta kālam kuḻintum niṟkum.
nac:
piṟakkaṭiyiṭā uṭaṉṟavēntaṉi uṉṉa marattuṭaṉ aṭukkik kūṟappaṭṭa uṉṉa nili eṉṟatu vēntaṉ karuttāṉaṉṟi avaṉ maṟavaṉ vēntaṟku nī veṟṟi koṭuttāl yāṉ niṉakku iṉṉatu ceyvaleṉap paravutalum, em vēntaṟku ākkam uḷateṉiṉ akkōṭu potuḷuka eṉavum, paki vēntaṟku ākkam uḷateṉiṉ akkōṭu paṭuvatāka eṉavum nimittam kōṭalum eṉa iruvakit teyvat taṉmi. aḵtuṭimiyāṉ aṭukkiya uṉṉaniliyum eṉṟār.
iraṇṭu niliyāṟ potuvumāyiṟṟu. maṉṉavaṉ veṟṟiyē karutātu iṅṅaṉam irunilimiyum karutaliṉ vaḻuvumāyiṟṟu.
ī.
nāvalar:
piṉvāṅkātu maliyum vēntaṉ veṟṟiyi uḷatteṇṇic cārttu vakiyāl uṉṉa marattil nimittam koḷḷum uṉṉa niliyum.
uṉṉam  ciṟṟiliyum poṟpūvum uḷḷatōr maravaki. paṇṭit tamiḻ maṟavar pōrkkeḻumuṉ uṉṉamarak kōṭṭil mālikaḷi aṭukki nimittam koḷḷuvatu vaḻakkāṟu. iṉik  kuṟipārppavar tam maṉṉaṟku ākkam eṉiṉ marakkōṭu taḻivatum, kēṭuḷatēl aḻivatum ākiya oru kaṭavuṭṭaṉmiyuṇṭeṉṟum ataṉāl porunar  pōrukkumuṉ ammarattip paracikkuṟi kēṭpareṉṟum avvāṟu kēṭṭalē uṉṉa nili eṉṟum urippārum uḷar.
u.
ā.ku:
uṉṉutal  eṇṇutal, tam eṇṇakkuṟippi veḷip paṭuttat takkateṉak karutappaṭṭa maram 'uṉṉam' ākalām. pūkkaṭṭip pārttal iṟivaṉ uṉṉam aṟitalām. iṉi uṉṉal nilipeṟu vīṟukāṭṭi niṉivil niṟṟalāl uṉṉam eṉappuṟattuṟi āyiṟṟām. ''maṉṉā ulakattu maṉṉutal kuṟittōr tam pukaḻ niṟīit tāmmāyntaṉarē" eṉpatu puṟappāṭṭu, uṉṉutal muṉṉutalāka viriyum.


ūrkoli
a.
''muṟṟiya ūrkoli"	(to.po.61)  (i.vi.603)
ā.
''ūrkoli"	(pu.ve.1)
viripari kaṭavi villuṭi maṟavar
kuriyaḻal naṭappak kuṟumpeṟit taṉtu	                   (pu.ve.1:7)
(veṭcittuṟikaḷuḷ oṉṟu)
i.
iḷam:
cūḻappaṭṭa ūri aḻittal.
nac:		nirikōṭaṟku eḻuntōr avarpuṟañcēriyi vaḷittuk koṇṭu āṇṭuniṉṟa nirikāvalarik koṉṟu paki yaṟuttalum niri mīṭṭaṟku eḻuntōr avvūri viṭṭuc ciṟṟūrik kāttuk kōṟalum.
ī.
nāvalar:
vaḷintukoṇṭu nirimīṭkap poruvārik kōṟal. ūr  mīṭkap porum ūravarkku ākupeyar.
ūrcceruvīḻntamaṟaṉ
a.
''ūrcceruvīḻnta maṟṟataṉ maṟaṉum"	              (to.po.69)
ā.	
arumiḷiyoṭu kiṭaṅkaḻiyāmic
cerumalinta ciṟappurit taṉṟu	(pu.ve.3)
''ūrcceru"	(pu.ve.3)
arumiḷiyoṭu kiṭaṅkaḻiyātu ikali
urukeḻu maṟavar uṭaṉṟa ūrcceruvum	                    (i.vi.609)
i.
iḷam:
ūrcceruviṉkaṇ vīḻnta pācimaṟaṉum.
nac:	
ammatiṟpuṟattaṉṟi ūrakattup pōri virumpiya pācimaṟaṉum pāci eṉṟār nīriṟpācipōla iruvarum otuṅkiyum  tūrntum porutaliṉ.
ī.
nāvalar:
atuvumaṉṟi, araṇakattu ūril amar virumpi oruvari oruvar muṉintu porum appācip pōriṉ taṟukaṇmiyum.


eccam
a.
colloṭum kuṟippoṭum muṭivukoḷ iyaṟki
pulliya kiḷavi eccam ākum	(to.po.507)
ā.
collē kuṟippē āyiraṇ ṭeccam	(i.a. 58)
muṟpaṭak kiḷanta poruṭpaṭik kellām
eccam āki varumvaḻi yaṟintu
kiḷanta alla vēṟupiṟa tōṉṟiṉum
kiḷantavaṟ ṟiyalāṉ uṇarntaṉar koḷalē	(i.a.59)
collē āyiṉum kuṟippē āyiṉum
colli muṭittal vēṇṭuva teccam	(na.a.232)
i.
iḷam:
(ceyyuḷ uṟuppukaḷuḷ) (eccavakiyāmāṟu uṇarttutal)
piṟitōr  colloṭum piṟitōr kuṟippoṭum  muṭivukoḷḷum iyaṟkiyip poruntiya  ceyyuḷ eccamākum eṉṟavāṟu.
eṉavē colleccam kuṟippeccameṉa iruvaki yāyiṉa. atu eccaviyaluḷ 'piriniliviṉi' yeṉṉum cūttirattuḷ (34) pirinili eṉpatu mutalākac collappaṭṭa eṇvakiyāṉum varuvaṉa col leccamām. kuṟippeṉṟu ōtappaṭṭatu kuṟippeccamām.
pērā:
(eccaṅkūṟukiṉṟatu).
kūṟṟiṉāṉum kuṟippiṉāṉum muṭikkappaṭum ilakkaṇattoṭu 'muṭivu koḷ iyaṟki' eṉavē, ceyyuḷiṉ kaṇṇataṉṟip piṉ koṇarntu muṭikkappaṭum eṉpatu peṟṟām.
''ceṅkaḷam paṭakkoṉ ṟavuṇart tēytta
ceṅkō lampiṉ ceṅkōṭ ṭiyāṉik
kaḻaltoṭic cēey kuṉṟam
kurutip pūviṉ kulikkān taṭṭē"
eṉac ceyyuṇ muṭintavaḻiyum ivaṟṟāṉ yāṅ kuṟiyuṭiyēm allēm eṉṟu talivaṟkuc coṉṉāḷēl atu kūṟṟeccamām. eṉṉi? avvāṟu kūṟavuñ  citinta tiṉmiyiṉ. talimakaṭkuc coṉṉāḷēl atu kuṟip peccam. eṉṉi? atu kāṇpāyākil kāṇeṉat talimakaḷi iṭattuyttu nīṅkiya kuṟippiṉaḷāki atutāṉ kūṟāḷākaliṉ eṉpatu.
nac:
(muṟiyē eccam eṉṉum uṟuppukkūṟukiṉṟatu).
kūṟṟiṉāṉum kuṟippiṉāṉum muṭikkappaṭum ilakkaṇattoṭu poruntiya kiḷavi eccam eṉṉum uṟuppām.
muṭivukoḷ iyaṟki eṉavē ceyyuṭ kaṇṇataṉṟip piṉkoṇarntu muṭittal peṟṟām.
''ceṅkaḷam... taṭṭē"
ikkāntaḷāl yām kuṟiyuṭiyam allam eṉat talivaṟkuk kūṟiṟ kūṟṟeccamām; akkūṟṟum ceyyuṭkuc citiviṉmiyiṉ. atu kāṇpāyākiṟ kāṇeṉat taliviyi nōkki iṭattuyttuk kūṟiṟ kuṟippeccamām; avaṉik kūṭuka eṉattāṉ kūṟāḷākaliṉ.
u.
ā.ku:	
eccam  eñciyiruppatu. takkār ''takavilar" eṉṉum tirukkuṟaḷil varum eccam ipporuṭṭatē. caṭukuṭu eṉṉum āṭṭattil pāṭic celpavaṉ pāṭuṅkāl iṭiyē mūccu viṭutali 'eccuppōtal'  eṉpatum, ''eccavaṉ iḷittavaṉ" eṉṉum iṇimoḻiyil varum 'eccavaṉ' eṉpatum ivvaḻip paṭṭaṉavē.
eṭṭuvaki nutaliya aviyam
a.
''eṭṭuvaki nutaliya aviyakat tāṉum"	              (to.po.75)
ā.
''aviya mulli"	(pu.ve.8)
''navi nīṅka naṭuvu kūṟum
 aviya māntar iyalpurit taṉṟu"	(pu.ve.8)
toṭiviṭi ūḻāt toṭiviṭi tuṉṉit
toṭiviṭi ūḻivi tōlāt  toṭivēṭṭu
aḻipaṭal āṟṟal aṟimuṟiyeṉ ṟeṭṭiṉ
vaḻipaṭartal valla tavi	(pu.ve.8)
kuṭippiṟappuk kalvi kuṇamvāymitūymi
naṭuccollu nallaṇi ākkam  keṭukkum
aḻukkā  ṟavāviṉmi avviraṇṭō ṭeṭṭum
iḻukkā aviyiṉkaṇ eṭṭu	(pu.ve.8 mēṟ.)
eṭṭuvaki nutaliya aviya mulli	                   (i.vi.613)
i.
iḷam:
eṭṭuppākupāṭṭik kuṟitta aviyakamum.
eṭṭuvaki kuṟitta aviyakam eṉṟamiyāṉ ēṉiya aviyiṉ ivvaki mikuti uṭittu eṉṟavāṟu. aviyāvaṉa: kuṭippiṟappu, kalvi, oḻukkam, vāymi, tūymi, naṭuvunilimi, aḻukkāṟāmi, avāviṉmi eṉpaṉa.
avi eṭṭiṉāṉum avi varumāṟu:
kuṭippiṟap puṭuttup paṉuval cūṭi
viḻuppēr oḻukkam pūṇṭu kāmuṟa
vāymi vāymaṭuttu māntit tūymiyiṟ	
kātal iṉpattut tūṅkit tītaṟu
naṭuvunili neṭunakar viki vikalum
aḻukkā ṟiṉmi avāviṉ miyeṉa
iruperu nitiyamum orutām īṭṭum
tōlā nāviṉ mēlōr pēravi.	     (āciriyamāli)
		(puṟattiraṭṭu, aviyaṟital)
nac:	
(pāṭam: aviyam).
eṇvakikkuṇattiṉik karutiya aviyattāratu nilimi yāṉum avi kuṭippiṟappu, kalvi, oḻukkam, vāymi, tūymi, naṭuvu nilimi, aḻukkāṟiṉmi, avāviṉmi, eṉa iviyuṭiyarāy avikkaṇ muntiyiruppōr veṟṟiyik kūṟutal.
''kuṭippiṟappuṭuttu" eṉa itaṉuḷ eṭṭum vantaṉa.
ī.
nāvalar:
eṇvakic cālpukaḷum niṟintār maṉṟattu matikkap peṟum ciṟappuṭiya aviyattu muntiyirukkum vīṟu kūṟukiṟatu. attakiya cālpukaḷ oḻukkam.... aḻukkāṟāmi eṉappaṭutal nūlāṉaṟintatu.


eṇ
a.
1.	vaṇṇakam tāṉē,
	taravē, tāḻici eṇṇē vārameṉ
	ṟannāl vakiyil tōṉṟum eṉpa.	(to.po.444)
2.	mutaṟṟoṭi perukic curuṅkumaṉ eṇṇē	(to.po.448)
3.	eṇṇiṭi oḻital ētam iṉṟē	(to.po.449)
ā.
īraṭi iraṇṭum ōraṭi nāṉkum
muccīr eṭṭum irucīr iraṭṭiyum
accīr kuṟiyiṉum ampō taraṅkam	(yā.vi.83 mēṟ.)
cērttiya taravoṭu tāḻicip piṉṉar
nīrttiri pōla neṟimiyiṉ curuṅki
mūvaki eṇṇum muṟimiyiṉ vaḻāa
aḷaviṉa vellām ampō taraṅkam	(ci.kā.)
uritta uṟuppoṭu tāḻicip piṉṉar
niritta aṭiyāṉ nīrttiri pōla
aciyaṭi peṟiṉavi ampō taraṅkam	                 (avinayam.)
muntiya tāḻicik kīṟāy muṟimuṟi
oṉṟiṉuk koṉṟu curuṅkum uṟuppiṉa
tampō taraṅkavot tāḻicik kaliyē	                    (yā.vi.83)
nīrttiripōl
marapoṉṟu nēraṭi muccīr kuṟaḷnaṭuvē maṭuppiṉ
......... ampō taraṅkot tāḻiciyē	                    (yā.kā.31)
āṅkataṉ iṭiyē aḷavaṭi cintaṭi
pāṅkami kuṟaḷaṭi paṭunīrt tiripōl
ōyntōyn tuṟṟiṭu māyiṉ aṉṉa
ampō taraṅka vottā ḻiciyum	(i.vi.738)
ampō taraṅkam ampaḷām tiripōl
aḷavaṭi īraṭi iraṇṭum pēreṇ;
aḷavaṭi ōraṭi nāṉkum aḷaveṇ;
cintaṭi ōraṭi eṭṭum iṭiyeṇ;
kuṟaḷaṭi ōraṭi nāṉāṉkum ciṟṟeṇ
eṭṭum nāṉāṉkum nāṉkum eṭṭumāyc
curuṅkavum annāl tuṇiyuṟup puṭittē          	                (to.vi.231)
i.
iḷam:
1.	(vaṇṇaka ottāḻicik kalippā āmāṟu uṇarttutal)
	vaṇṇaka ottāḻiciyāvatu taravum tāḻiciyum eṇṇum curitakamum eṉṟu collappaṭṭa nāṉku uṟuppiṉiyum uṭittu eṉṟavāṟu.
2.	(eṇṇāmāṟu uṇarttutal).
	mutaṟṟoṭutta uṟuppup perukippiṉ toṭukkum uṟuppuc curuṅki varum eṉṟavāṟu.
	ataṉi iraṇṭaṭiyāṉ varuvaṉa iraṇṭum, ōraṭiyāṉ varuvaṉa nāṉkum, cintaṭiyāṉ varuvaṉa eṭṭum, kuṟaḷaṭiyāṉ varuvaṉa patiṉāṟum eṉappiṟa nūlāciriyar urippar. ivvāciriyarkku variyaṟi ilavām.
3.	(mēlataṟkōr puṟanaṭi uṇarttutal)
	mēṟcollappaṭṭa eṇ orōoṉṟu iṭiyoḻintu varutal kuṟṟamākātu, taṉiccol illāvaḻi eṉṟavāṟu.
	eṉavē collappaṭṭa uṟuppukkaḷ taṉiccol varuvaḻi iṭi yoḻiyāmal varutal vēṇṭum eṉṟavāṟu. taṉiccol uḷappaṭa  aintuṟuppāyiṟṟu.
	iṉit taṉiccol iṉṟi eṇ iṭiyiṭṭavaḻi 'orupōku' eṉappeyar peṟum.
pērā:
1.  eṇṇuṟupputtāṉ nīrttiripōla varavarac curuṅki varutaliṉ ampōtaraṅkam eṉavum amiyum.
2.	eṇṇuṟuppāmāṟu uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
	mutal perukiyavaḻi mutaleṇ.
	'toṭi' eṉṟataṉāṉ taliyeṇ iraṇṭu aḷavaṭiyāṉ oru toṭiyāki varumeṉavum, 'peruki' eṉṟataṉāṉ iraṇ ṭaṭiyāṉ iraṇṭaṉi nāṭṭa atuvēpaṟṟi, mutal perukiya eṉṟataṉāṉ oḻinta eṇṇuttoṭi peṟutalum nērntāṉām. itaṉi, mutaleṉavē vaḻimuṟi varuvaṉavum eṇṇaveṉpatu  peṟṟām. avi, ittuṇi eṉpatu aṟiyumā ṟeṉpateṉṉi?
'mutaṟṟoṭi peruki' eṉṟataṉāṉ iraṇṭaṭiyāṉ iraṇṭaṉi nāṭṭi atuvē paṟṟic curuṅkum eṉṟamiyiṉ īraṭiyiṟ curuṅki ōraṭiyātalum, ōraṭiyiṟ curuṅki iru cīrātalum,  irucīriṟ curuṅki orucīr ātalum koḷḷappaṭum; 'nāṟcīrkoṇṭa taṭiyeṉap paṭumē' eṉṟāṉākaliṉ.
ākavē irucīr eṉappaṭṭatu kuṟaḷaṭiyāyiṉum ivi iraṇṭu toṭarn taṉṟi etukiyāy amiyāmiyiṟ kuṟaḷaṭi eṉṉāṉ āyiṉāṉ eṉpatu.
orucīrkkum aḷavaṭippaṭuttut toṭikōṭal okkum. eṉṉi? aḷavaṭippaṭuttē toṭikoḷpavākaliṉ.
aḵtēl orucīriṉum curuṅkappeṟātō eṉiṉ   kantaruva nūliṉkaṇṇum orucīriṟ curuṅkiṉa vārāvākaliṉ ivaṉum 'piṟanūl muṭintatu  tāṉuṭampaṭutal' eṉṉum uttivakiyāṉ orucīriṟ curuṅkutal nērāṉ eṉpatu. maṟṟu vaḻimuṟiyāṟ pākañ curuṅkutal peṟumāṟu eṉṉi eṉiṉ,
'vakāra miciyum makāraṅ kuṟukum' (puḷḷi. 35) eṉṟāṟpōla mēlik koṇṭum varukiṉṟavāṟṟāṟ pākamē curuṅkutal peṟalām eṉpatu. maṉ eṉpatu ākkattiṉ kaṇṇum vantatu ākaliṟ curuṅkip palavām eṉpatu koḷka.
eṉavē, iraṇṭaṭi iraṇṭum, ōraṭi nāṉkum, irucīr eṭṭum, orucīr patiṉāṟumāki eṇpalkum eṉpatu. irucīrkuṟaḷaṭiyu mākaliṉ orucīrāṉ varuvaṉa ciṟṟeṇ eṉavē paṭum. ākavē, oḻinta eṇmūṉṟum tali yeṇṇum iṭiyeṇṇum kaṭiyeṇṇum eṉa mūṉṟum kūṭṭi eṇṇi niṟṟaṟ kuriyavāyiṉa. itu nōkkip pōlum eṇṇeṉṟu aṭakkātu, 'ciṉṉa mallākkāli' eṉa oru cīriṉi vēṟupaṭuttumēṟ kūṟukiṉṟateṉpatu.
iṉi, aḷavaṭiyiṉi nāṭṭiyē mutaṟṟoṭi perukic curuṅkum eṉṟamiyiṉ, aḷavaṭiyiṟ curuṅkiṉa irucīrum orōvaḻic ciṉṉa meṉappaṭum. iṉi allātār īraṭi iraṇṭiṉip pēreṇ eṉavum, ōraṭi nāṉkiṉic ciṟṟeṇ eṉavum, kaṭi eṇṇiṉi iṭiyeṇ eṉavum, orucīrāṉ varuvaṉavaṟṟi aḷaveṇ eṉavuñ collupa. aḷavaṭiyāka nōkkip pēreṇ ciṟṟeṇ eṉṟalum ciṉṉam paṭṭavaḻi iṭiniṉṟatu iṭiyeṇ ātalum elli aḷavittākiya ciṉṉam aḷaveṇṇātalum amiyum. avaṟṟuḷ irucīriṉi  muccīrākavum orucīriṉi irucīrākavum alaku vippi ṉum ataṉi, aciyum cīrum iciyoṭu cērtti eṉpataṉāṉ maṟukka.
3.	(mēlataṟkōr puṟaṉaṭi).
muṟkūṟiya eṇkaḷiṉṟi varutal ceyyuṭku ētamiṉṟu. ciṉṉa eṇṇoṉṟu moḻintu nillāviṭattu.
eṉavē, ciṉṉa eṇ oḻiyātu mūvaki eṇṇum oḻitalum, ciṉṉa eṇ oḻiya mūvaki eṇṇum peṟutal peritum cuviyuṭittu eṉṟavā ṟāyiṟṟu. itaṉāṉē 'eṇṇoḻi tal' eṇṇātu 'iṭi' eṉṟataṉāl taliyeṇṇum iṭiyeṇṇum allaṉa eṭṭu nāṉkākiyum, patiṉāṟu eṭṭākiyum kuṟintu varum eṉpatum koḷka. mūvaki eṇṇum ciṉṉamum peṟṟu varutal ciṟap puṭimi. 'ētamiṉṟu' eṉṟataṉāṟ peṟutum.
nac:
1.	(eṇṇuṟuppu) nīrttiripōl muṟiyē curuṅki varutaliṉ ataṉi ampōtaraṅka ottāḻici eṉṟum, vaṇṇakam eṉṟataṉi arāka vuṟuppākki avvuṟuppuṭiyatu vaṇṇaka ottāḻiciyeṉṟum kūṟuvārumuḷar. itu piṉṉuḷḷōr kūṟiya nūṟkellām mutaṉūlāta liṉ, tolkāppiyaṉāri māṟupaṭutal marapaṉṟeṉka.
2.	(eṇṇuṟuppāmāṟu uṇarttukiṉṟatu).
	eṇṇāvatu muṟpaṭap perukiya vaḻimuṟiyāṟ curuṅki varappeṟum 'toṭi; eṉṟataṉāṉ oḻinta eṇkaḷum toṭipeṟutalum nōkkārāyiṟṟu. 'muṟpaṭa' eṉavē piṟpaṭac cilavarum eṉpatu peṟṟām.
	peruki eṉṟataṉāṉ īraṭiyāṉ iraṇṭināṭṭi vaḻimuṟi curuṅki varum eṉavē īraṭiyiṟ curuṅki ōraṭiyāyum, ōraṭiyiṟ curuṅki iru cīrāyum, irucīriṟ curuṅki oru cīrāyum oṉṟaṟkoṉṟu pākamē curuṅkutal koḷka.
	'nāṟcīr koṇṭa taṭiyeṉappaṭum' (cey.32) eṉṟaliṉ aḷavaṭiyē koḷka. irucīrāṉ varuvaṉa iraṇ ṭiṇintu aḷavaṭi yātaṟkēṟpat toṭikoṇṭu cuvaṭupaṭa niṟṟalāṉum, orucīrāṉ varuvaṉavum nāṉkiṇintu aḷavaṭiyātaṟkēṟpat toṭikoṇṭu cuvaṭupaṭa niṟṟalāṉum aviyum aḷavaṭikkaṇ aṭaṅkiṉa. maṉ ākkamāta liṟ curuṅkiyum palavātal koḷka. īraṭiyum ōraṭiyum irucīrum orucīrumāyc  curuṅki vārā niṟkavum, iraṇṭiṟku nāṉkum, nāṉkiṟku  eṭṭum, eṭṭiṟkup patiṉāṟumāy oṉṟaṟkoṉṟu uṟuppu vakiyāṉ iraṭṭikkum eṉak koḷka. irucīr varuvaṉa kuṟaḷaṭi eṉap paṭutaliṉ mēṟciṉṉameṉpaṉa aṭiyiṉ aṭaṅkā orucīrēyāyiṉa.
	ākavē itaṉi oḻinta eṇ mūṉṟum nāṉkumāyt taliyeṇ iṭiyeṇ kaṭiyeṇ eṉavum paṭutal nōkkimēl eṇṇum ciṉṉamum eṉappirittōtupa.
	iṉi aḷavaṭiyiṟ curuṅkum eṉṟaliṉ ivvaḷavaṭiyiṟ curuṅkiya irucīriyum oruvaḻic ciṉṉameṉavumpaṭum. iṉi īraṭi iraṇṭiṉip pēreṇ eṉavum, ōraṭiyataṉiṟ kuṟitaliṟ ciṟṟeṇ eṉavum,  ivaṟṟiṟkum piṉvarum  ciṉṉattiṟkum iṭiyēniṟṟaliṉ irucīri iraṇ ṭiṭiyeṇ eṉavum muṭiviṟku aḷavāy niṟkum ciṉṉattiṉi aḷaveṇ eṉavum peyar kūṟiṉum amiyum. irucīriṉi muccīrākkiyum orucīriṉi irucīrākkiyum alakuvip piṉ ataṟku aciyum cīrum iciyoṭu cērāmi uṇarka.
3.	(mēlataṟkōr puṟaṉaṭi)
	muṟkūṟiya eṇkaḷiṉṟi varutal ceyyuṭku ētamiṉṟu, ciṉṉa eṇṇoṉṟu nillāta iṭattu.
	eṉavē ciṉṉa eṇṇoḻiyātu mūvakiyeṇṇum oḻitalum, ciṉṉa eṇṇoḻiyumiṭattu mūvaki yeṇṇum  oḻiyātu varuvatum cuviyuṭittāyiṟṟu.
	iṭiyeṉṟataṉāṟ ṟaliyeṇṇum iṭiyeṇṇum allaṉa eṭṭu nāṉkākiyum, patiṉāṟu eṭṭākiyum kuṟintu varutalum koḷka. mūvaki eṇṇum ciṉṉamum peṟṟu varutal ciṟap puṭimi, ētamiṉ ṟeṉpataṉāṟ peṟutum.
u.
ā.ku:
ampōtaraṅka orupōku kāṇka.


eṇṇappaṭā eḻuttu
a.
uyiril leḻuttum eṇṇap paṭāa
uyirttiṟam iyakkam iṉmi yāṉa	      (to.po.351)
ā.
oṟṟaḷa peḻāvaḻip peṟṟaala kilavē	(kā.pā.)
aḷapeḻiṉ allati āytamum oṟṟum
alakiyal peytā eṉmaṉār pulavar	(avinayam)
taṉinili oṟṟivi tāmala kilavē
aḷapeṭi allāk kāli yāṉa	(yā.vi.3. mē)
		(yā.kā.38. mē)
īroṟ  ṟāyiṉum mūvoṟ ṟāyiṉum
ōroṟ ṟiyala eṉmaṉār pulavar	(yā.vi.3. mēṟ.)			           (yā.kā.38. mē.)
oṟṟaḷa peḻāvaḻip peṟṟaala kiṉmiyum	(i.vi.742)
i.
iḷam:
(aṭikkuriya eḻuttu variyaṟi uṇarttutal).
uyirillāta eḻuttum eṇṇappaṭā; uyirpōla iyakka miṉmiyāṉ, ummi eccavummiyātalāṟ  kuṟukiya vuyirttākiya kuṟṟiyalikaramum kuṟṟiyalukaramum eṇṇap paṭā eṉṟu koḷka. eṉavē eṇṇappaṭuvaṉa uyirum uyir meyyumāki oru māttiriyiṟ kuṟiyātaṉa eṉṟu koḷḷappaṭum.
pērā:
(eḻutteṇṇic cīrvakutta muṟiyāṉē oṟṟum āytamum kuṟṟu karamum oruṅkeṇṇappaṭutal eytiṉavaṟṟi vilakkiyavāṟu).
tattam ōci iṉitu viḷaṅkat tattam taṉmiyāṉa olittaṟṟoḻil illāta eḻuttukkaḷ īṇṭeṇṇappaṭā; aṅṅaṉam eṇṇappaṭāta vum eḻutteṉappaṭutaliṟ ciṟitu nāppuṭi peyarun tuṇiyāṉ olittalum moḻi  cārntu olittalum  uṭiyavaṉṟē! aṅṅaṉam oru vāṟṟāṉ uyirkkun tiṟamuṭiyavāyiṉum av vuyirkkum tiṟam īṇṭuc ceyyuṭpāṟpaṭuṅkāl upakārappaṭa iyaṅkumāṟila vākaliṉ eṇṇappaṭā.
mēṟcīrtaḷi irunilimippaṭutta atikārattāṉ oṟṟum kuṟṟu karamumē īṇṭu vilakkiṉāṉ eṉpatu iccūttiram valittatāyiṟṟu, ''oṟṟeḻut tiyaṟṟē kuṟṟiya likaram" (cey.8) eṉavum mēṟkūṟiṉāṉ ātaliṉ eṉpatu.
nac:
oṟṟum āytamum kuṟṟukaramum eḻutteṉappeyar kūṟiṉārēṉum eḻuttāka eṇṇappaṭā; eṉṉi? avi tattam ōci iṉitu viḷaṅka olittaṟkuk kūṟiya eṇvaki nilattiṉum viḷaṅka iyaṅkāmiyiṉ.
eṉṟatu, eḻutteṇṇumiṭattu avi oḻitteṇṇuka eṉṟavāṟu. itu kaṭṭaḷiyaṭikku, ''pērntu ceṉṟu" eṉpataṉuḷ avi oḻittu eṇṇap paṭṭatu. piṉ ṉuḷḷōr koṇṭa kaṭṭaḷik kalittuṟi, cantam tāṇṭakaṅkaṭkum ivvāṟeḻutteṇṇutal vēṇṭu meṉṟuṇarka.
eṇṇāṉku poruḷum, nāṉāṉku poruḷum
a.
''paṇṇit tōṉṟiya eṇṇāṉku poruḷum
kaṇṇiya puṟaṉē nāṉāṉ keṉpa"	(to.po.245)
i.
iḷam:
(cuviyum cuvikkuṟippum uṇarttutal)
viḷiyāṭṭāyattiṉkaṇ tōṉṟiya muppattiraṇṭu poruḷiyum kuṟittu ataṉpuṟattu nikaḻum poruḷ patiṉāṟu eṉṟu colluvar pulavar.
paṇṇiyuṭiyatu paṇṇiyeṉṟār; puṟattu nikaḻvataṉip puṟameṉṟār. 
paṇṇit tōṉṟiya kaṇṇiyapuṟaṉ eṉap peyarecca aṭukkākak kūṭṭuka.  aṉṟiyum, eṇṇāṉku poruḷuṅ kaṇṇiyapuṟaṉ eṉa orucol  naṭiyāka oṭṭit tōṉṟiya eṉṉum peyareccattiṟku muṭipākkiṉum amiyum. puṟaṉ eṉṉum eḻuvāy nāṉāṉkeṉṉum payaṉili koṇṭu muṭintatu.
īṇṭuc collappaṭṭa patiṉāṟu poruḷum kaṟṟu nalloḻukku oḻukum aṟivuṭiyār avikkaṇ tōṉṟāmiyāṟ paṇṇit tōṉṟiya eṉṟār. eṉṉi?  nakikkuk kāraṇamākiya eḷḷal avarkaṇ tōṉṟāmiyiṉ. piṟavum aṉṉa.
muppattiraṇṭāvaṉa: naki mutalāṉavaṟṟiṟkētuvām eḷḷal mutalāka viḷiyāṭṭīṟāka muṉṉeṭuttu ōtappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟik kuṟitta puṟaṉāvaṉa cuviyum kuṟippum.
vīram, accam, iḻippu, viyappu, kāmam, avalam, uruttiram, naki, naṭuvunilimi eṉṟum, vīrakkuṟippu, accakkuṟippu, iḻippuk kuṟippu, viyappukkuṟippu, kāmak kuṟippu, avalakkuṟippu, uruttirak kuṟippu, nakikkuṟippu, naṭuvunilimik kuṟippu eṉṟum collap paṭṭa patiṉeṭṭiṉum naṭuvuniliyum ataṉ kuṟippum oḻitta patiṉāṟumām.
'viyappu' eṉiṉum 'aṟputam' eṉiṉum okkum.
'kāmam' eṉiṉum 'ciruṅkāram' eṉiṉum okkum.
'avalam' eṉiṉum 'karuṇi' eṉiṉum okkum.
'uruttiram' eṉiṉum 'vekuḷi' eṉiṉum okkum.
vīram eṉpatu māṟṟārik kuṟittu nikaḻvatu.
accam eṉpatu, añcat takuvaṉa kaṇṭavaḻi nikaḻvatu.
iḻippu eṉpatu, iḻikkattakkaṉa kaṇṭavaḻi nikaḻvatu.
viyappu eṉpatu, viyakkattakkaṉa kaṇṭuḻi nikaḻvatu.
kāmam eṉpatu, iṉpa nikaḻcciyāṉ nikaḻvatu.
avalam eṉpatu, iḻavu paṟṟip piṟappatu.
uruttiram eṉpatu, avamatippāṟ piṟappatu.
naki eṉpatu ikaḻcci mutalāyiṉavaṟṟāṟ piṟappatu.
naṭuvunilimi eṉpatu, yātoṉṟum vikārappaṭāmi. avi iṟṟāka mattimam eṉpataṉi īṇṭoḻittatu eṉṉi yeṉiṉ,
''mattimam eṉpatu mācaṟat teriyiṟ
collap paṭṭa ellāc cuviyoṭum
pullā tākiya poliviṟ ṟeṉpa"
''nayaṉuṭi marapiṉ itaṉpayam yāteṉiṟ	
cērtti yōrkkum cārntupaṭu vōrkkum
oppa niṟkum niliyiṟ ṟeṉpa."
''uyppō ritaṉi yāreṉiṉ mikkatu	
payakkun tāpatar cāraṇar camaṇar
kayakkaṟu muṉivar aṟivaroṭu piṟarum
kāmam vekuḷi mayakkam nīṅkiya
vāymi yāḷar vakuttaṉar piṟarum
accuvi orutali ātaliṉ ataṉi
avarkkila ātaliṉ
meyttalip paṭukkavitaṉ mikavaṟin tōrē"
eṉpatu ceyiṟṟiyac cūttiram. itaṉāṉē itu vaḻak kilakkaṇam aṉṟu eṉa uṇarka.
iṉi, cuvi eṉpatu kāṇappaṭu poruḷāṟ  kāṇpōr  akattiṉ varuvatōr vikāram.
''iruvaki nilattiṉ iyalvatu cuviyē"
eṉṟum,
''niṉṟa cuviyē...
oṉṟiya nikaḻcci cattuvam eṉpa"
eṉṟum,
''cattuvam eṉpatu cāṟṟuṅ kāli
meymmayir kuḷittal  kaṇṇīr vārtal
naṭukkaṅ kaṭuttal viyarttal tēṟṟam
koṭuṅkuraṟ citivoṭu niralpaṭa vanta
patteṉa moḻipa cattuvan tāṉē"
eṉṟum cārporuḷ urippa.
pēyāṉum puliyāṉum kaṇṭāṉ oruvaṉ añciyavaḻi mayakkamum karattalum naṭukkamum viyarppumuḷavākiṉṟē. avaṟṟuḷ accat tiṟkētuvākiya puliyum pēyum cuvippaṭu poruḷ. avaṟṟik kaṇṭa kālan toṭṭu nīṅkātu niṉṟa accam cuvi. ataṉkaṇ mayakkamum karattalum kuṟippu. naṭukkamum viyarppum cattuvam. ivaṟṟuḷ  naṭukkamum viyarppum piṟarkkum pulaṉāvatu eṉṟu koḷka. ēṉiya maṉanikaḻcci. piṟavumaṉṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ virivi nāṭaka nūliṟ kāṇka.
pērā:
(meyppāṭu piṟar vēṇṭumāṟṟāṉ ittuṇip pakutippaṭu meṉṟuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu).
muṭiyuṭi vēntarum kuṟunila maṉṉarum mutalāyiṉōr nāṭaka makaḷir āṭalum pāṭalum kaṇṭum kēṭṭum tām nukarum  iṉpa viḷiyāṭṭi ṉuḷ tōṉṟiya muppattiraṇṭu poruḷum avi karutiya poruṭpakuti patiṉāṟāki aṭaṅkum nāṭaka nūlāciriyarkku.
atu mutaṉūli nōkkik kūṟiyavāṟu pōlum. muppattiraṇṭāvaṉa yāvi eṉiṉ, oṉpatu cuvi eṉappaṭṭaṉavaṟṟuḷ uruttiram oḻinta  eṭṭaṉiyum kūṟuṅkāṟ cuvikkappaṭum poruḷum, ataṉi nukarnta  poṟiyuṇarvum, atu maṉattup paṭṭavaḻi uḷḷattu nikaḻum kuṟippum, kuṟippukkaḷ piṟanta uḷḷattāṟ kaṇṇīr arumpalum, meymmayir cilirttalum mutalāka uṭampiṉkaṇ varum vēṟupāṭākiya cattuvaṅ kaḷumeṉa nāṉkākki accuvi eṭṭōṭuṅ kūṭṭi oṉṟu nāṉku ceytu uṟaḻa muppattiraṇṭām eṉpatu. eṉavē, cuvipporuḷum cuvi yuṇarvum kuṟippum viṟalumeṉa nāṉkāyiṉa. viṟal eṉiṉum cattuvam eṉiṉum okkum.
cuvipporuḷ eṉpaṉa, aṟucuvikku mutalākiya vēmpum  kaṭuvum uppum puḷiyum pākkum, karumpum pōlvaṉa.  aviyāmāṟu: nakic cuvikkup poruḷāvaṉa āriyar kūṟum tamiḻum kuruṭarum muṭavarum cellum celavum pittarum kaḷiyarum cuṟṟattāri ikaḻntalum kuḻavikūṟum  maḻaliyum pōlvaṉa.
accap poruḷāvaṉa:
''vaḷḷeyiṟ ṟarimā vāḷvari vēṅki
  muḷḷeyiṟ ṟaravē muḻaṅkaḻaṟ centī
  īṟṟā matamā ēka pātam
  kūṟṟam kōṇmā kuṉṟuṟi yacuṇam"
eṉṟu collappaṭṭaṉa pōlvaṉa. ivaṟṟic cuvi kōṭal eṉpatu eṉṉi eṉiṉ, nakiyum accamum mutalākiya uṇarvu muṟkālattu ulakiyalāṉ aṟivāṉ oruvaṉ avaṟṟukku ētuvākiya poruḷpiṟa kaṇṭavaḻit tōṉṟiya poṟiyuṇarvukaḷ avvaccuvi eṉappaṭum. vēmpeṉṉum  poruḷum nāveṉṉum poṟiyum talippeytuḻi yallatu kippuc cuvi piṟavātatu pōla ap poruḷ kaṇṭavaḻiyallatu nakiyum accamum tōṉṟā. oḻinta kāmam mutaliyaṉavum aṉṉa.  ikkaruttē paṟṟip piṟkālattu nāṭakanūl ceyta āciriyarum 'iruvaki nilattiṉ iyalvatu cuviyē' eṉṟār eṉpatu.
iṉi, iruvaki nilaṉeṉpaṉa uyppōṉ ceytatu kāṇpōrkku eytutal aṉṟō eṉiṉ, cuvi eṉpatu oppiṉāṉ āyapeya rākalāṉ vēmpu cuvittavaṉ aṟinta kippaṟiviṉi nāvuṇar viṉāṟ piṟaṉuṇarāṉ, ivaṉ kippuc cuvittāṉeṉak kaṇṇuṇarviṉāṉ aṟiva taṉṟi; atupōla accattukku ētuvākiya oru poruḷ kaṇṭu añci ōṭi varukiṉṟāṉ oruvaṉi maṟṟoruvaṉ kaṇṭa vaḻi ivaṉ vaḷḷeyiṟṟarimā mutalāyiṉa kaṇṭu añciṉāṉ eṉṟaṟivatallatu vaḷḷeyiṟṟarimāviṉit tāṉkāṇṭal vēṇṭuvataṉṟu; tāṉkaṇ ṭāṉāyiṉ, atuvum cuviyeṉavē paṭum. ākavē añciṉāṉik kaṇṭu nakutalum karuṇi ceytalum kaṇṭōrkkup piṟappataṉṟi accam piṟavātākalāṉ uyppōṉ ceytatu kāṇpōṉuytta aṟiviṉ peṟṟiyāṟ cellātākaliṉ iruvaki nilameṉappaṭuvaṉa cuvipporu ḷum cuvittōṉu meṉa irunilattum nikaḻum eṉpatē poruḷātal vēṇṭum eṉpatu.
kuṟippeṉpatu, kippiṉ cuviyuṇarvu piṟantavaḻi veṟuppu mutalāyiṉa uḷḷa nikaḻccipōla, añcutakkaṉa kaṇṭavaḻi ataṉi nōkkātu veṟukkum uḷḷa nikaḻcci. viṟal eṉpaṉa, avvuḷḷa nikaḻcci  piṟantavaḻi vēmpu tiṉṟārkkut talinaṭuṅkuvatu pōlat tāmē tōṉṟum naṭukkam mutalāyiṉa. ivvakiyāl innāṉkiṉiyum eṭṭoṭum uṟaḻavē muppattiraṇ ṭāyiṉa.
iṉi, avi patiṉāṟu āyiṉavāṟu eṉṉi eṉiṉ, vēmpu mutalā yiṉa poruḷum ataṉoṭu nāmutalāyiṉa poṟiyum vēṟuvēṟu niṉṟavaḻic cuviyeṉṟu colvatē piṟavāmiyāṉum avviraṇṭuṅ kūṭiyavaḻic cuviyeṉpatu piṟattalāṉum avi patiṉāṟum eṭṭeṉappaṭum;  iṉik kuṟippum cattuvamum eṉpaṉavum uḷḷa nikaḻcciyum uṭampiṉ vēṟu pāṭum eṉparākaliṉ avvuḷḷa nikaḻcciyi  veḷippaṭuppatu cattuva mākaliṉ  avi īreṭṭup patiṉāṟākum eṉpatu maṟṟivi paṇṇit tōṉṟuvaṉa vāyiṉ, itu poruḷōttiṉuḷ  ārāyvateṉṉi?  nāṭaka vaḻakkantāṉē, oruvaṉ ceytataṉi oruvaṉ  vaḻakkiṉiṉṟum vāṅkik koṇṭu piṉṉarc ceykiṉṟatākalāṉum vaḻakkeṉappaṭātu ākalāṉum īṇṭu ārāyvatu piṟiteṭutturittal eṉṉum kuṟṟamām eṉpatu kaṭā. atuvaṉṟē iccūttiram piṟaṉkōḷ kūṟaleṉṉum uttivakiyāṟkūṟi marapāyiṟṟeṉpatu.
a.
''eṇṇuvaṇṇam eṇṇup payilum"	            (to.po.529)
ā.	
''eṇ"	 (vī.cō.142)
''eṇṇuvaṇṇam"	(to.vi.250 i.vi.757)
''eṇṇi varuvaṉa eṇṇeṉap paṭumē"	              (mu.vī.987)


eṇṇuvaṇṇam
i.
iḷam:
(eṇṇuvaṇṇam uṇarttutal)
eṇṇup payiṉṟu varuvatu eṇṇu vaṇṇamām.
eṭu:
''nilam nīr vaḷivicum peṉṟa nāṉkiṉ
aḷappari yiyē"
eṉavarum.
pērā:
eṇṇup payilvatu eṇṇuvaṇṇam.
iḵtu aṭiyeṇṇup payiṟalāṉ eṇṇuvaṇṇameṉak kāraṇap peyarāyiṟṟu.
''naṉṉaṉ ēṟṟi naṟumpū ṇatti
tuṉṉaruṅ kaṭuntiṟaṟ kaṅkaṉ kaṭṭi"
eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa.
nac:
eṇṇu vaṇṇamāvatu aṭikkaṇṇē payiṉṟu varuvatu. itu kāraṇap peyar.
''naṉṉaṉ ēṟṟi... kaṭṭi"
eṉa varum.
ī.
yā.vi:
eṇṇuvaṇṇam eṉpatu cevveṇṇiṉālum, ummi eṇṇiṉā lum, eṉa eṇṇiṉālum eṉṟā eṇṇiṉālum piṟavum yātāṉum ōr eṇṇiṉālum varuvatu.


etuki
a.
1.	mōṉi etuki muraṇē iyipeṉa
	nālneṟi marapiṉa toṭivaki eṉpa.	            (to.po.393)
2.	aḵtoḻit toṉṟiṉ etuki ākum.	            (to.po.398)
3.	āyiru toṭikkum kiḷiyeḻut turiya	            (to.po.399)
ā.
iraṇṭām eḻuttoṉ ṟiyivatē etuki	  (yā.vi.36)
iraṇṭām vaḻuvā eḻuttoṉṟiṉ ... etuki	(yā.kā.16)
ēṉṟā mutalaḷa vottiraṇ ṭāmeḻuttoṉṟivariṉ
cāṉṟār ataṉi etukiyeṉ ṟōtuvar	                 (vī.cō.111)
ēṉi, iraṇṭām eḻuttō ṭiyiyiṉ etukiyum	(i.vi.723)
etuki eṉpa tiyivaṉa moḻikkaṇ
mutaleḻut taḷavottu mutaloḻit toṉṟutal	(to.vi.213)
iraṇṭām eḻuttoṉ ṟuvatetu kiyē	               (mu.vī.yā.26)
i.
iḷam:
1.	(toṭippākupāṭuṇarttutal).
	(etukiyāmāṟuṇarttutal).
	aṭitoṟum mutaleḻuttoṉṟāmal iraṇṭāmeḻut toṉṟiṉ etukiyākum.
3.	(eytiyataṉmēl ciṟappuviti yuṇarttal).
	mōṉit  toṭikkum etukit toṭikkum  eṭutta eḻuttē varutalaṉṟi varukka eḻuttum uriya eṉṟavāṟu.
	''āṟaṟi antaṇark karumaṟi palapakarntu
	  tēṟunīr caṭikkarantu"	(kalit.kaṭavuḷ)
pērā:
1.	(tūkkuṇarttit toṭivaki uṇarttuvāṉ eḻuntāṉ. avitām iviyeṉap peyarum muṟiyum tokiyum uṇarttutal).
2.	(iraṇṭām eṇṇu muṟimikkaṇ niṉṟa etukit toṭi yuṇarttutal).
	iraṇṭaṭiyiṉum mutaṉiṉṟa cīr mutaleḻuttiraṇṭum oḻiya allaṉa vellām  oṉṟi varutalum orō veḻuttē oṉṟi varutalumeṉa iṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟiyavaṟṟuḷ aḵtoḻittu accīr muḻutoṉṟil taliyāketuki yeṉṟum, iraṇṭāmeḻut toṉṟil aṭiyā ketuki yeṉṟum vaḻaṅkappaṭum.
	ayaleḻuttoṉṟiṉ eṉṉātu mutaleḻuttiṉic cuṭṭik kūṟutal eṉṉi eṉiṉ, ivvāṟu etukiyāṅkāl mutaleḻuttukkaḷiṉ māttiri tammiṉ ottu varal vēṇṭumeṉṟaṟkuk kūṟiṉāṉ eṉpatu.
	(aṭiyetukiyi) iṭiyāketukiyeṉavum amiyum. oḻintaṉa kaṭiyāketuki eṉappaṭum.
3.	(mēṟkūṟiya toṭippāṟ paṭṭuvaruvaṉa vēṟupala toṭikaṇṭu avi kūṟukiṉṟatu).
āyiru toṭi eṉpaṉa mōṉiyum etukiyum, ivi iraṇṭaṟkum kiḷiyeḻuttukkaḷum oṉṟi varappeṟum.
itaṉ karuttu, aṅṅaṉaṅ kiḷiyāki vanta vēṟupāṭṭāṉ ellāt toṭiyum vēṟu palavām eṉṟavāṟu.
kiḷiyeḻut teṉpaṉa, varukkamum neṭilum aṉuvum valliṉamum melliṉamum iṭiyiṉamum uyirumeṉa ivi. iviyellām orē kiḷimiyuṭimiyiṟ kiḷiyeṉappaṭṭatu.
iṉi, ācetukiyum mūṉṟāmeḻuttoṉ ṟetukiyum aṭiyetukik kurittākiya iraṇṭāmeḻuttiṉ muṉṉum piṉṉum toṭarntu  niṟṟaliṉ aviyum kiḷiyeṉiṉum  amiyum; aṟṟaṉṟu;  aṭimōṉi yum taliyāketukiyum aṭiyetukiyum vantu, nunti eṉṉum cīrkkaṇ mūṉṟā meḻuttoṉṟi varutal 'meypeṟu marapiṟ ṟoṭi' eṉappaṭ ṭamiyiṉ. āṇṭu atu kiḷiyākātu. meyttal ottal eṉṟa vaḻiyaṉṟi, ītal  kāytal eṉṟavaḻi neṭiṉ mōṉiyāyt  tiripupaṭutaliṉ āciṭi etukiyum kiḷiyākātu.
īṇṭu ōtiya kiḷi uyirum oṟṟumeṉa iraṇṭākaliṉ  avviru vāṟṟāṉum kiḷimipaṟṟi aṉukkoḷḷappaṭum. eṉṉi? 'aā āyiraṇ ṭaṅkāntiyalum'  akara ākāram kiḷiyāyiṉa.
aikāra aukāram pōli vakiyāṉ avi eṉappaṭum.
i ī e ē eṉpaṉavum u ū o ō eṉpaṉavum uṭaṉpiṟappiṉavākalāṉ aviyum kiḷi eṉappaṭum.
oṟṟukkiḷi eṉpaṉa tammuṉṉart tām varutalum piṟavarutalumeṉa iraṇṭu. avaṟṟuḷ tam muṉṉarttām varuṅkāl vanta uyirē vārā. avvaṉuviṟku ōtiya uyirē vēṟupaṭa varumeṉpatu.
iṉi ōraṭikkaṇṇē aiñcīrāṉum aṟucīrāṉum vanta vaḻiyiṉum aṉuveṉappaṭum eṉpatu. iṉit tam muṉṉākap piṟa varuṅkāl vanta uyirē varavum peṟumeṉak koḷka. piṟa varuṅkāl ñakara  nakaraṅkaḷum, makara vakaraṅkaḷum, cakara takaraṅkaḷum iṉam paṟṟik kiḷiyām eṉpatu. avaṟṟuḷ  makara vakaraṅkaḷ itaḻpaṟṟip piṟattaliṟ kiḷiyāyiṉa. ivvāṟē i ī e ē eṉpaṉavum yakaramum miṭaṟṟuvaḷiyāṟ piṟattalāṉum  iyalpop pumiyāṉum kiḷiyām eṉpatu.
nac:
1.	(niṟuttamuṟiyāṉē toṭikūṟukiṉṟār; avaṟṟatu peyarum muṟiyum uṇarttutal).
2.	(iḵtu etuki kūṟukiṉṟatu).
muṟkūṟiya mutaleḻuttiṉi oḻittu iraṇṭaṭiyiṉum, cīrmuḻutum oṉṟiṉum, iraṇṭāmeḻuttē oṉṟiṉum, ōraṭikkaṇ mutaliru cīrkkaṇ iraṇṭāmeḻuttu oṉṟiṉum, mutaṟcīriṉum mūṉṟām cīriṉum iraṇṭā meḻuttu oṉṟiṉum,  mutaṟcīriṉum nāṉkām cīriṉum iraṇṭā meḻuttu oṉṟiṉum, mutaṟcīroḻittu ēṉiya oṉṟiṉum, mutalayaṟcīroḻittu ēṉiya oṉṟiṉum īṟṟayaṟ cīroḻittu ēṉiya oṉṟiṉum, nāṟcīrum oṉṟiṉum taliyā ketuki, aṭiyetuki, iṇiyetuki, poḻip petuki, orūu etuki, kūḻi etuki, mēṟkatuvāyetuki, kīḻkkatuvā yetuki, muṟṟetuki eṉak kūṟappaṭum.
ayaleḻuttoṉṟiṉ eṉṉātu mutaleḻuttic cuṭṭiya ataṉāl, mutaleḻuttiṉ māttiri tammuḷ ottal vēṇṭum eṉṟuṅ koḷka.
3.	(itu muṟkūṟiyavaṟṟōṭu oppaṉa cilatoṭi kūṟukiṉṟatu).
muṟkūṟiya mōṉikkum etukikkum kiḷi yeḻuttukkaḷum oṉṟivarap peṟum.
kiḷiyeḻuttāvaṉa: varukkamōṉiyum, varukka vetukiyum, valliṉa vetukiyum, melliṉa vetukiyum, iṭiyiṉa vetukiyum eṉa aintām.
ī.
yā.vi:
etukit toṭiyiṟ cīr muḻutum varuvatu taliyāku etuki; ōreḻuttē varattoṭuppatu iṭiyāku etuki; iṉattāṉum māttiriyāṉum piṟavāṟṟāṉum varattoṭuppatu kaṭiyāku etuki;  eṉṟum, muṉ  iraṇṭaṭiyum ōr etukit toṭiyāy vantu, piṉ iraṇṭaṭiyum maṟṟoru tiṟattāṉ variṉum kuṟṟamilli. aḵtu iraṇṭaṭi etuki, eṉpār orucār āciriyar  eṉṟaṟkum,  orucār āciriyar etukit toṭiyuḷ ya, ra,la, ḻa eṉṉum nāṉku oṟṟum vantu mikat toṭuttāl ataṉi āciṭi etuki eṉṟu vēṇṭuvar eṉpatu aṟivittaṟkum vēṇṭappaṭṭatu.	 (yā.vi.37)
u.
ā.ku:
etuki vakikaḷiṉ viri
mutaleḻut  taḷavot tayaleḻut toṉṟuva
tetuki ataṉvaḻi iyiyavum peṟumē
mutaleḻut toṉṟuva mōṉi; etuki
mutaleḻut taḷavō ṭottatu mutalā
atuvoḻit toṉṟiṉ ākum eṉpa.	                  (palkāyam)
mutaleḻut toṉṟiṉ mōṉi yākum
aḵtoḻit toṉṟiṉ etuki yākum	
āyiru toṭikkum iṇiyeḻut turiya	                   (nattattam)
mutaleḻut toṉṟi muṭivatu mōṉi
ēṉiya toṉṟiṉ etukit toṭiyē
uṟuppiṉ oṉṟiṉ vikaṟpamum appāl
neṟippaṭa vantaṉa nērappaṭumē	(ciṟukā.)
varukka neṭiliṉam variyār āṇṭē	(yā.vi.37)
aṭitoṟum mutaleḻut tāvati mutaṟṟoṭi
iṭiyataṉ muṉṉoṉ ṟiyivati etuki
neṭiya piṟavum iṉattiṉum ākum
cīrmuḻu toṉṟiṉ taliyā ketuki
ōreḻut toṉṟiṉ iṭikaṭi piṟavē.
yaralaḻa veṉṉum īriraṇ ṭoṟṟum
varaṉmuṟi piṟaḻātu vantiṭi uyirppiṉ
āciṭi etukiyeṉ ṟaṟintaṉar koḷalē	          (yā.vi.37 mēṟ.)
iraṇṭaṭi etuki tiraṇṭoruṅ kiyaṉṟapiṉ
muraṇṭa etuki iraṇṭala variyār
kaṭiyiṇi piṉmuraṇ iṭippuṇar muraṇeṉa
iviyuṅ kūṟupa orucā rōrē.	(yā.vi.39 mēṟ.)
varukka neṭiliṉam vantāl etukiyum mōṉiyumeṉ
ṟorukkap peyarāl urikkap paṭumuyir āciṭiyiṭ
ṭirukkum orucār iraṇṭaṭimūṉṟām eḻuttoṉṟiṉ
nirakkum etukiyeṉ ṟālum ciṟappila nēriḻiyē	(yā.kā.42)
taliyā ketuki taṉcīr muḻutuṟal
iṭikaṭi avvav veḻuttoṉ ṟuvatē         	                (to.vi.213)
mūṉṟām eḻuttoṉṟal āciṉam taliyāku
iṭikaṭi āṟum etuki vakiyē.	(to.vi.214)
varukka neṭiliṉam vantāl etukiyum
mōṉiyu māmeṉa moḻiyap paṭumē.	                   (mu.vī.978)
yaralaḻa iṭiyuṟiṉ āceṉap paṭumē	(mu.vī.979)


eruttu
a.
1.	koccakam arākam curitakam eruttoṭu
	ceppiya nāṉkum taṉakkuṟup pākak
	kāmaṅ kaṇṇiya nilimit tākum.	(to.po.426)
2.	eruttē koccam arākam ciṟṟeṇ
	aṭakkiyal vāramō ṭannilik kurittē.	            (to.po.455)
i.
iḷam:
1.	(paripāṭalāmāṟu uṇarttutal)
	erutteṉpatu iraṇṭaṭi iḻipākap pattaṭip perumiyāka varuvatōr uṟuppu. pāṭṭiṟku mukam taravātalāṉum kāl curitaka mātalāṉum iṭinilip pāṭṭākiya tāḻiciyum koccakamum arākamuṅ koḷḷak kiṭattaliṉ erutteṉpatu kaḻuttiṉ puṟattiṟkup  peyarāka vēṇṭumātalāṉ avvuṟupput taravic cārntu kiṭattal vēṇṭumeṉṟu koḷka.
	''taravē eruttam arākam koccakam
	  aṭakkiyal vakiyō ṭintuṟup puṭittē"
	eṉpatu akattiyamātaliṉ. taraveṉpatōr uṟuppuṅ kōṭal vēṇṭumeṉiṉ  ivvāciriyar koccakam eṉa ōtiya ataṉāṉē taravum avvilakkaṇattiṟpaṭumeṉpatu oṉṟu. eruttu eṉpatu ivvāciriyar karuttiṉāl taraveṉpatu pōlum.
2.	(ampōtaraṅkattiṟku uṟuppāmāṟu uṇarttutal)


īṇṭu eruttu eṉpatu taravu
pērā:
1.	(paripāṭaṟkēṟṟa pāvuṟuppiṉi eṭuttu virikūṟi ittuṇi uḷḷuṟuppuṭit teṉkiṉṟatu).
	eruttiṉi īṟṟukkaṇ  vittāṉ ataṉi iṉṟiyum varum paripāṭal eṉṟaṟkum, iḵtu iṭivaru meṉṟaṟkum eṉpatu.
2.	(ampōtaraṅka vuṟuppu ivi aintumeṉak kūṟiyavāṟu).
	taraveṉiṉum erutteṉiṉum okkum.
nac:
1.	(paripāṭaṟkēṟṟa uḷḷuṟuppuk kūṟukiṉṟatu. ivi ciṟappuṟuppākiya muppattu nāṉkumaṉṟi itaṟkum kalikkum uṟuppāy varumeṉṟu koḷka).
	eruttiṉi īṟṟil vittatu taraviṉṟiyum atu (paripāṭal) varutaliṉ.
2.	(ampōtaraṅkavuṟuppu ivi aintumeṉak kūṟiyavāṟu).
	taraveṉiṉum eruttameṉiṉum okkum.
ī.
yā.vi:
'taravu' eṉiṉum 'eruttam' eṉiṉum okkum.
ellā uṟuppiṉ poruḷiyum tokuttuk koṇṭu tantumuṉ niṟṟaliṉ taravu eṉpatūum kāraṇakkuṟi.
''tantu muṉ niṟṟaliṉ taravā kummē".	                    (yā.vi.82)
taṉṉuṭi antamum tāḻici ātiyum
tuṉṉu miṭattut tuṇintatu pōlici
taṉṉoṭu niṟṟal taravuk kiyalpē	(kā.pā.)


eḻuttiyal vaki
a.
avaṟṟuḷ,
mēṟkiḷan taṉavē eṉmaṉār pulavar.	(to.po.311)
ā.
kuṟilum neṭilum aḷapeṭiyum oṟṟum
aṟiñar acikkuṟuppām eṉpar  vaṟitē
uyirmeyyum mūviṉameṉ ṟōtiṉar eṉṟu
ceyiravarkku niṉṟatō ceṉṟu.
neṭiya kuṟiya uyirmey uyirum
valiya meliya iṭimi aḷapeṭi
mūvuyirk kuṟukkamō ṭāmacik keḻuttē	                   (avinayam)
kuṟilneṭil aḷapeṭi uyiruṟup puyirmey
valiya meliya iṭimiyō ṭāytam
iu aiyeṉa mūṉṟaṉ kuṟukkamō
ṭappatiṉ mūṉṟum acikkuṟup pākum	(kā.pā.)
kuṟiya neṭiya uyiruṟup puyirmey
valiya meliya iṭimi yaḷapeṭi
mūvuyirk kuṟukkamum āmacik keḻuttē	                  (ci.kā.pā.)
kuṟiluyir valleḻuttuk kuṟṟukara āti
kuṟukiya aiaumav vāytam  neṟimiyāl
āynta acitoṭitām vaṇṇaṅkaṭ keṇmuṟiyāl
ēyntaṉa annāṉ keḻuttu.
kuṟilneṭil āytam aḷapeṭi aikārak
kuṟilkuṟ ṟikara vukaram  maṟuvil
uyirmey virāymeyyō  ṭāṟā ṟeḻuttum
ceyirvaṉmi meṉmi camaṉ	(nālaṭināṟpatu)
uyiruṟuppuyirmey  taṉinilieṉāak,
kuṟilneṭil aḷapeṭi mūviṉam eṉāa
aḵkiya nalluyir maḵkāṉ kuṟukkamō
ṭintu taliyiṭṭa aiyī reḻuttum
acitoṭi nēraci yākum uṟuppeṉa
vaciyaṟu pulavar vakutturit taṉarē	              (periyapammam)
uyirē meyyē uyirmey yeṉṟā
kuṟilē neṭilē aḷapeṭi eṉṟā
vaṉmi meṉmi iṭimi eṉṟā
cārpil tōṉṟum taṉmiya eṉṟā
aiau makarak kuṟukkam eṉṟāṅ
kimmū veḻuttum āmacik kuṟuppē	(yā.vi.2)
kuṟilneṭil āvi kuṟukiya mūvuyi rāytameyyē
maṟuvaṟu mūviṉam mitīr uyirmey matimaruṭṭum
ciṟunutal pēramarkkaṭceyya vāyiya nuṇṇiṭiyāy
aṟiña ruritta aḷapum acikkuṟup pāvaṉavē	      (yā.kā.4)
avaṟṟuḷ,
eḻuttumēṟ kiḷanta iyalpiṟ ṟākum	(i.vi.712)
i.
iḷam:	
eḻuttiyalāvatu, uyireḻuttu meyyeḻuttu cārpeḻuttu eṉa mūvakippaṭum.
uyireḻuttu, kuṟṟeḻuttu neṭṭeḻuttu aḷapeṭi eṉa mūvakip paṭum.  meyyeḻuttu, valliṉam melliṉam iṭiyiṉam eṉa mūvakip paṭum. cārpeḻuttu, kuṟṟiya likaram, kuṟṟiyalukaram āytam eṉa mūvakip paṭum.  meyyiṉuṭ cilavum āytamum aḷapeṭukkap peṟum.
kuṟṟeḻuttu a, i, u, e, o; neṭṭeḻuttu ā, ī, ū, ē, ai, ō, au; aḷapeṭi āa, īi, ūu, ēe, aii, ōo, auu valliṉam kacaṭatapaṟa; melliṉam ṅañaṇanamaṉa; iṭiyiṉam yaralavaḻaḷa; kuṟṟiyalukaramāvatu neṭ ṭeḻuttiṉ piṉṉarum mūṉṟeḻuttu muṉṉāṉa moḻiyiṉum valleḻutti ūrntu vanta ukaram nāku  nākku; kāṟu  kāṟṟu eṉa innikaraṉa.
kuṟṟiya likaramāvatu, ivvukaran tirintu makaramūrntu makaramōṭiyintu varum. nākiyātu ukarantirintatu. kēṇmiyā makaramūrntatu; piṟavum aṉṉa.
āytamāvatu kuṟṟeḻuttiṟkum valleḻuttiṟkum iṭivarum. aḵtāvatu eḵku eṉavarum.
oṟṟaḷapeṭiyāvatu melliṉamum vayalaḷavum āytamum aḷapeṭuk kum. avi maṅṅkalam, maññcu eṉavarum.
iṉi uyirum meyyum kūṭi uyirmeyyeḻuttām. avi kakara mutal ṉakara vīṟākiya irunūṟṟorupattāṟum iṉṉum aikārak kuṟukkamum makarak kuṟukkamum eṉpavumuḷa.
aikārakkuṟukkam aḷapeṭiyun taṉiyumallāta vaḻik kuṟukum. makarak kuṟukkam ṇakara ṉakara oṟṟiṉ piṉvarum. puṇarmoḻik kaṇ vakarattiṉ mēṉiṉṟa makaraṅkuṟukum. iviyellām eḻuttatikārattuṭ kāṇka.
pērā:
īṇṭup patiṉintu eḻutteṉṟu kūṟip payaṅkōṭumāyiṉ āṇṭi muppattu mūṉṟeḻuttiṉ vēṟupaṭap piṟantaṉa cilaveḻuttu malla eṉpāṉ mēṟkiḷantaṉṉa eṉṟāṉ eṉpatu.	
maṟṟu eḻuttiyal vakiyiṉi māṭṭēṟṟāṉ muppattu mūṉṟeṉak koḷvataṉṟip patiṉinteṉṟu koḷḷumā ṟeṉṉiyeṉiṉ eḻuttōttiṉuḷ kuṟilum neṭilum uyirum meyyum  iṉa mūṉṟum cārpeḻuttu mūṉṟumeṉap pattum iyalpu vakiyāṉ āṇṭup paṭuttōtiṉāṉ, uyirmeyyum uyiraḷa peṭiyum tattam vakiyāṟ kūṭumāṟum aikāram aukāram pōlivakiyāṟ kūṭumāṟum yāḻ nūlakattu oṟṟici nīḷumāṟum āṇṭut tōṟṟuvāy ceytāṉ. ceyyavē, avi īṇṭuk kūṟum eḻuttiyal vakiyōṭokku meṉṟu uyttuṇarntu koḷḷa vittāṉ eṉpatu. ivaṟṟoṭu makarakkuṟukkamum kūṭṭip patiṉāṟeḻuttu eṉpārumuḷar.  ataṉāṟ payaṉeṉṉi yeṉiṉ, pāṭṭuṭit  talivaṉ kēṭṭukkuk kāraṇamāmeṉpar. maṟṟu uyirmeyt toṭakkattu aintaṉiyum mēl eḻutteṉṟilaṉākalāṉ īṇṭu eḻuttiyal vakiyuḷ eḻuttākki aṭakkumāṟeṉṉiyeṉiṉ  īṇṭu aṉṉa veṉavē āṇṭu iraṇṭeḻuttiṉ kūṭṭameṉavum moḻiyeṉavum pōli yeṉavum kūṟiṉāṉāyiṉum avaṟṟi eḻuttiyal vakiyeṉap peyar koṭuppavē āṇṭu niṉṟa vakiyāṉē īṇṭu eḻut teṉappaṭum eṉpatāyiṟṟu.
itaṉ karuttu iyaleṉṟataṉāṉ iyaṟṟik koḷḷum vakiyāṉ eḻuttu iṉiyaveṉṟāṉām. vaki eṉpataṉāṉ muppattu mūṉṟiṉik kuṟilum neṭilum eṉṟaṟṟoṭakkattup peyar vēṟupāṭṭāṟ pattu vakippaṭa iyaṟṟutalum kūṭṭavakiyāṉ iraṇṭum pōlivakiyāṉ iraṇṭum yāḻnūl vēṇṭum vakiyāṉ oṉṟu meṉa aintu vakiyāṉ  iyaṟṟutalu meṉa  iruvakiyum koḷḷappaṭum  allatū um ceyyuṭkaḷ av veḻuttu vakiyāṉ iṉṉōciyavāka virāyc ceytalum koḷka. maṟṟu, aḷapeṭiyi mēl moḻiyeṉṟāṉ īṇṭeḻut teṉṟāṉāka liṉ, itaṉi ōreḻutteṉṟu kōṭumō iraṇ ṭeḻut teṉṟu  kōṭumō eṉiṉ, ''neṭṭeḻuttimpar  ottakuṟ ṟeḻuttē" eṉṟu kūṟiyavāṟṟāṉ āṇṭiraṇṭeḻutteṉak koḷḷak kiṭantatāyiṉum muṉṉarp pōkki, ''aḷapeṭi  acinili ākalu murittē" eṉṟavaḻi eḻuttu nilimiyum eytuvikkumākalāṉ etiratu nōkki īṇṭu ōreḻutteṉṟu kōṭum eṉpatu.
iṉi avaṟṟuṭ kuṟilum neṭilum kuṟṟukaramum acik kuṟuppām. maṟṟu, ''oṟṟoṭu  varutaloṭu meyppaṭa nāṭi" eṉpataṉāṉ oṟṟum acikkuṟuppākāvō eṉiṉ, accūttirattāṉaṉṟē avi kuṟilum neṭilum aṭuttu vantum vēṟupaṭātu nērniriyātal eytiyatu nōkki avi acikkuṟuppākā eṉṟateṉka. maṟṟu, iṭiniṉṟu niriyātali vilakkāvō eṉiṉ aṉṉatoru vitiyuṇṭāyiṉaṉṟē ivi iṭi pukuntu vilakka vēṇṭuvateṉa maṟukka.
neṭilum aḷapeṭiyiraṇṭum uyirum uyirmeyyum valliṉamum melliṉamum iṭiyiṉamum aikārak kuṟukkamum aukārakkuṟukkamum eṉappattum toṭikku uṟuppām.
kuṟilum neṭilum aḷapeṭi iraṇṭum iṉammūṉṟum āytamum vaṇṇattiṟku uṟuppām.
iṅṅaṉamē vēṟupaṭavanta payaṉōkki eḻuttiṉi iyaṟṟik kōṭaliṉ eḻuttiyal vakiyeṉṟāṉ eṉpatu. aḵtēl, oṟṟum kuṟṟukaramum īṇṭōtiya teṉṉiyeṉiṉ  oṟṟaḷapeṭiyāṉ aciniliyum cīrniliyum kōṭaliṉ īṇṭu oṟṟuk kūṟiṉāṉ. kuṟṟikaramum uyirāyi ṉum  oṟṟuppōla eḻutteṇṇi aṭivakukkuṅkāl eṇṇappaṭā teṉṟaṟkuk kūṟiṉāṉ eṉpatu.
aḵtēl, ''oṟṟeḻut tiyaṟṟē kuṟṟiya likaram" eṉpavākaliṉ, ati īṇṭut taḻāaṟka eṉiṉ ak karuttiṉāl avvāṟum amiyum eṉpatu. aṟṟaṉṟu itaṉi mēṟ puḷḷipeṟumeṉṟilaṉ ātaliṉ puḷḷi peṟum  eṉṟaṟkum 'oṟṟeḻuttiyaṟṟē kuṟṟiya likaram' eṉṟāṉ eṉpatu.
iṉi, ''eḻuttiyal vakiyum mēṟkiḷantaṉṉa" eṉpataṉiyum eḻut tilakkaṇattil tiriyāmal ceyyuḷ ceyka eṉṟavāṟu eṉpaveṉiṉ  aḵtē karuttāyiṟ collōttiṉuḷḷum eḻuttilakkaṇamākiya mayakkamum niliyum mutalākiya ilakkaṇam tiriyāmal collārāytal vēṇṭum eṉavum, ivvatikārattuḷḷum eḻuttiyal  vakiyum colliyal vakiyum mēṟkiḷantaṉṉa eṉavum cūttirañ ceyvāṉmaṉ āciriyaṉeṉa maṟukka.
nac:
muṟkūṟiya muppattu mūṉṟeḻuttum yāppilakkaṇat tiṟkup patiṉintu peyaravāy naṭakkum kūṟupāṭu(m) eḻuttōttiṟ kūṟiya ilakkaṇattil piṟaḻāmi varumeṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar.
eḻuttu muppattu mūṉṟaṉuṭ cila eḻuttukkaḷi uyir eṉṉum peyar koṭuttu avaṟṟik kuṟilum neṭilum aḷa peṭiyum kuṟṟiyalikaramum kuṟṟiyalukaramum aikārak kuṟukkamum aukārak kuṟukkamum  eṉappeyar vēṟupāṭu koṭuttu ataṉōṭu eṭṭākkiyum cila eḻuttukkali meyyeṉṉum peyar koṭuttu avaṟṟi melliṉamum valliṉamum iṭiyiṉamum āytamum oṟṟaḷa peṭiyum eṉap peyar vēṟupāṭu koṭuttu ataṉōṭu āṟākkiyum iviyiraṇṭum kūṭiyavaṟṟi uyirmey eṉa vēṟōr peyarākkiyum mēṟkūṟiyavāṟē patiṉintu peyaravāy īṇṭu naṭakkum eṉṟaṟku 'eḻuttiyalvaki' eṉṟār.
ivveḻuttiṉi eḻuttōttiṉuṭ kūṟiya cūttiraṅkaḷoṭu māṭṭeṟin tār. avi, 'aukāra viṟuvāy' (nūṉ.8), 'a i, u. e (nū.3) 'ā ī ū ē' (. 4), 'nīṭṭam vēṇṭiṉ' (. 6), avitām, kuṟṟiyalikaram (. 2), 'ōraḷapākum iṭaṉumāruṇṭē' (moḻi. 4); 'valleḻutteṉpa' (eḻut.19), 'melleḻutteṉpa' (.20), 'iṭiyeḻutteṉpa' (.21), 'uyirmey yallaṉa' (moḻi.27), eṉpaṉavām. aḷapeṭi yiraṇṭum  eḻuttāntaṉmi mēlē (cey. 17, 18) peṟutum.
ivaṟṟuṭ kuṟilum neṭilum kuṟṟukaramum acik kuṟuppām.
oṟṟaṭutta acikaḷ vēṟupaṭāmiyiṉ oṟṟu acikku uṟuppākā. oṟṟaḷapeṭikku uṟuppām.
neṭilum aḷapeṭi iraṇṭum uyirum uyirmeyyum mūviṉamum aikāra aukārakkuṟukkamum toṭik kuṟuppām.
kuṟilum neṭilum aḷapeṭi iraṇṭum mūviṉamum āytamum vaṇṇat tiṟku uṟuppām.
ivaṟṟi iyaṟki eḻuttum cārpeḻuttum eṉa iraṇṭākavum vakuppar.


eḻutiṇi
a.
kikkiḷi mutalāp peruntiṇi iṟuvāy
muṟpaṭak kiḷanta eḻutiṇi eṉpa.	(to.po.1)
kikkiḷi mutalā ēḻperun tiṇiyum
muṟkiḷan taṉavē muṟiyi ṉāṉa.	(to.po.486)
ā.
malartali ulakattup pulavōr āynta
aruntamiḻ akapporuḷ kikkiḷi aintiṇi
peruntiṇi eṉaveḻu peṟṟit tākum.	(na.a.1)
kikkiḷi yuṭiya torutalik kāmam
aintiṇi yuṭiya taṉpuṭik kāmam
peruntiṇi eṉpatu poruntāk kāmam
atuvē kikkiḷi aintiṇi ēṉip
peruntiṇi eṉaveḻu peṟṟit tākum.	                    (i.vi.376)
kikkiḷi aintiṇi peruntiṇi muṟiyē
orutalik kāmamum aṉpuṭik kāmamum
poruntāk kāmamum poruntutal uṭiya	(i.vi.377)
akapporuḷ puṟapporuḷ āmiraṇ ṭavaṟṟuḷ
perukiya kikkiḷi peruntiṇi kuṟiñci
ātiyin tiṇiyeṉa akapporuḷ  ēḻē.	(to.vi.199)
akapporuḷ kikkiḷi aintiṇi peruntiṇi
eṉeḻu vakippaṭum eṉmaṉār pulavar.	               (mu.vī.aka.1)
avaṟṟuḷ kikkiḷi orutalik kāmam
aṉpuṭik kāmamin tiṇivayiṉ paṭumē	(mu.vī.aka.3)
poruntāk kāmam peruntiṇip poruṭṭē	(mu.vī.aka.5)
i.
iḷam:
kikkiḷi eṉṟu collappaṭum poruḷ mutalākap peruntiṇi eṉṟu collappaṭum poruḷ īṟāka  ēḻuporuḷ muṟpaṭak kūṟappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟu colvar.
avveḻu tiṇiyum āvaṉa; kikkiḷi, mulli, kuṟiñci, pāli, marutam, neytal, peruntiṇi.
innūlakattu oruvaṉum oruttiyum nukarum kāmattiṟkuk kulaṉum kuṇaṉum celvamum oḻukkamum iḷamiyum aṉpum oruṅku uḷavaḻi iṉpam uḷatām eṉavum, kikkiḷi, orutali vēṭki eṉavum, peruntiṇi ovvāk kūṭṭamāy iṉpam payattal aritu eṉavum kūṟutalāṉ innūluṭiyār kāmattup payaṉiṉmi uyttu uṇara vittavāṟu aṟintukoḷka.
nac:
kikkiḷi eṉappaṭṭa oḻukkam mutalākap peruntiṇi yeṉṉum oḻukkattiṉi iṟutiyākavuṭiya ēḻaṉiyum muṟpaṭak kūṟappaṭṭa akattiṇi ēḻeṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.
mutalum īṟum kūṟittiṇiyēḻeṉavē naṭuvaṇin tiṇi uḷavātal peṟutum. avimēṟkūṟupa. (akat.3).
ī.
nāvalar:
kikkiḷi  mutalākap peruntiṇi iṟutiyāka muṉṉē collap paṭṭa akattiṇi ēḻāmeṉṟu kūṟuvar, tamiḻnūl vallār.
iccūttirattil akattiṇi ēḻum niralē kūṟap peṟāmi yāṉum kiḷakkum eṉṉātu kiḷanta eṉa iṟantakāla eccam  peyta peṟṟimi yāṉum. itaṉ piṟakuḷḷa cūttira vippu muṟiyil kikkiḷiyi mutalākavum ataṉi aṭuttu aṉpiṉ aintiṇiyum iṟutiyiṟ peruntiṇiyumāka amittuk kūṟappeṟāmal mutaṟkaṇ aṉpiṉ aintiṇi kūṟi, avaṟṟiṉpiṉ kikkiḷi peruntiṇikaḷ toṭarntu kūṟappaṭuvatālum muṟpaṭak kiḷanta eḻutiṇi eṉpatu akattiṇi yiyalil  iccūttirattiṉ piṉ amittuk kūṟappaṭṭa muṟiyic cuṭṭāteṉpatu veḷippaṭi. eṉavē,  īṇṭu 'muṟpaṭak kiḷanta' eṉpatu iṭattāl muṟpaṭak kūṟum amippu muṟiyōṭu poruntāmiyāl kālattāl muṟpaṭak kiḷanta oṉṟaṉiyē kuṟikkum eṉpatu tēṟṟamākum.
mu.a:
kikkiḷi mutalāp peruntiṇi iṟuvāy eṉa mutalum īṟum kūṟi aṭuttu varum nūṟpāvil 'naṭuvaṇ aintiṇi' yeṉak kuṟittuḷḷa miyāl akattiṇi yēḻum māṭṭeṟintu piṉṉark kiḷakkappaṭum puṟattiṇikaḷ ēḻuḷa veṉpavi aṟivittaṟkē 'muṟpaṭak kiḷanta eḻutiṇi'  eṉa iṟanta kālattāṟ kūṟiṉār eṉṟu iḷam pūraṇarum nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum poruḷ koṇṭaṉar; ivvāṟu poruḷ koḷvatil muṉṉeṉpatu kālamuṉṉākavē niṉṟatu. avarkaḷ itaṉi iṭamuṉṉākak koṇṭu poruḷ kūṟāmiyāl ivviya lil piṉvarum nūṟpākkaḷil kikkiḷi aintiṇi peruntiṇi yeṉṉum muṟippaṭi ilakkaṇam kūṟappaṭavilli eṉpatu poruntātu. aṅṅaṉam muṟi piṟaḻak kūṟiyataṟkuk kāraṇam naṭuvaṇin tiṇiyiṉ ciṟappum, ēṉiyavaṟṟiṉ ciṟappiṉmiyumē yākum.
'kikkiḷi mutalāp peruntiṇi yiṟuvāy' eṉa mutalum īṟuṅkūṟi 'eḻutiṇi'yeṉat toki koṭuttamiyāl iv viraṇṭaṟkum  iṭiyil  aintiṇikaḷ uḷa veṉpatum, ivveḻu tiṇikaḷum akattiṇiyiyal toṭakkattil kūṟappaṭṭiruttalāl akattiṇikaḷē eṉpatum peṟappaṭṭaṉa.
paṭutiri viyattiṉ pala pakutikaḷil vāḻum palatiṟappaṭṭa āṭavar makaḷiriṭiyē nikaḻum kātaliṉpa vuṟavupaṟṟiya oḻukalāṟukaḷ ellām ikkikkiḷi mutaliya eḻutiṇiyuḷ aṭaṅkuvaṉavākum. paṭutiri viyam eṉpatu etiratu pōṟṟalāl (nūṟpā.2) iṅku eṭuttāḷap paṭṭatu.
eḻunilatteḻunta ceyyuḷ
a.
pāṭṭuri nūlē vāymoḻi piciyē
aṅkatam mutucol avvēḻ nilattum
vaṇpukaḻ mūvar taṇpoḻil varippiṉ
nāṟpē relli akattavar vaḻaṅkum
yāppiṉ vaḻiya teṉmaṉār pulavar.	(to.po.384)
ā.
ōraṭi yāṉum palavaṭi yāṉum
orōvaḻi iyalum urittaac ceyyuḷ
avitām,
pāṭṭuri nūlē mantiram piciyē
mutucol aṅkatam vāḻttoṭu piṟavum
ākkiṉa eṉpa aṟintici ṉōrē	(palkāyam.)
avitām,
pāṭṭuri nūlē mantiram piciyē
mutucol aṅkatam vāḻttoṭu piṟavum
ākum eṉpa aṟinticiṉōrē.	(i.vi.760)
i.
iḷam:
pāṭṭumutalāka mutucol īṟākac collappaṭṭa eḻunilattiṉum vaḷaviya pukaḻiyuṭiya cēraṉ pāṇṭiyaṉ cōḻaṉ eṉṉum mūvaratu tamiḻnāṭṭakattavar vaḻaṅkum toṭarmoḻikkaṇ varum yāppāvatu eṉṟavāṟu.
eṉavē, yāppāvatu, pāṭṭiyāppu, uriyāppu, nūliyāppu, moḻi yāppu, piciyāppu, aṅkatayāppu, paḻamoḻi yāppu eṉa eḻuvakip paṭum.
mēliccūttirattuḷ, 'kuṟitta poruḷi muṭiya nāṭṭal' eṉṟami yāṉum, iccūttirattuḷ, 'nāṟpē relli yakattavar vaḻaṅkum yāppiṉ vaḻiyatu' eṉṟu ōtiṉami yāṉum, kuṟitta poruḷ muṭiyumāṟu coṟ ṟoṭuttal eṉṟu koḷḷappaṭum.
pērā:	pāṭṭum uriyum nūlum vāymoḻiyum piciyum aṅkatamum mutu collumeṉṟu collappaṭṭa avvēḻu nilattum, ''vaṭavēṅkaṭan teṉkumari yāyiṭi" nāṭṭār  naṭāttukiṉṟa ceyyuḷiṉ peyarē taṉakkup peyarākavu muṭittu mēṟkūṟiya yāppu.
eṉavē, mēṟkuṟitta poruḷi aṭikkaṇ muṭiya nāṭṭal yāp peṉṟāṉ. atuvēyaṉṟi ippakutippaṭac ceytalum yāppuṟuppē yeṉṟāṉām. ivvēḻu vakiyāṉum ceytaṉ marapeṉa etiratu nōkkiṟṟu.
nac:
(yāppiṟkōr vēṟupāṭu kūṟukiṉṟatu).
pāṭṭukkaḷum urikaḷum nūlkaḷum mantiraṅkaḷum picikaḷum aṅkata mum mutucollum eṉappaṭṭa avvēḻu poruḷiṭattum koṭiyāṟ peṟṟa pukaḻiṉiyuṭiya cēracōḻa pāṇṭiyaruṭiya kuḷircciyiyuṭiya nāvalam poḻiluḷ tamakku varintu koṇṭa vaṭavēṅkaṭan teṉkumari yiṭattu mali maṇṭalam, cōḻa maṇṭalam, pāṇṭiya maṇṭalam, toṇṭi maṇṭalam eṉṉum  nāṉku peyariyuṭiya tamiḻ nāṭṭār naṭāttum ceyyuḷiṉ peyar taṉakkup peyarākavum uṭittu mēṟkūṟiya yāppu eṉṟavāṟu.
eṉṟatu, kuṟitta poruḷi aṭikkaṇ muṭiya nāṭṭutalē yaṉṟi ip pakutippaṭac ceytalum yāppuṟup peṉṟatām. pāṭṭuc ceyyuḷ uric ceyyuḷ nūṟceyyuḷ eṉattamiḻ nāṭṭār vaḻaṅkiyavāṟē peyar peṟu meṉṟavāṟu. eṉavē annāṭṭār vaḻaṅkum yāppup pakuti kūṟiṉā rāyiṟṟu.
u.
ā.ku:
eḻunilam  ēḻupakuti. 'vaṭavēṅkaṭam... nallulakattu' eṉappaṉam pāraṉār nūliṉ  iṭavarampu kūṟiyataṟku vittu innūṟpā ākalām. uriyum oruvaki yāppuṭiyatu eṉiṉ piṟavelām yāppuṭimi kūṟa vēṇṭuva tilliyām. pāṭṭu, uri eṉa varuvaṉavaṟṟic ciṟapput talippukaḷuḷ kāṇka.


ēntalvaṇṇam
a.
ēntalvaṇṇam, colliya colliṟ colliyatu ciṟakkum	(to.po.532)
ā.
nēriṟu uriccīr mikavan tēntiya
ōciyiṉvaruvaṉa ēntici yākum.	(mu.vī.990)
i.
iḷam:
ēntal vaṇṇamāvatu colliya colliṉāṉē collappaṭṭatu ciṟakkavarum.
kūṭuvār kūṭalkaḷ kūṭal eṉappaṭā
kūṭaluṭ kūṭalē kūṭalum  kūṭal
arumpiya mulli arumpaviḻ mālip
piriviṟ pirivē pirivu
eṉa varum.
pērā:
colliya collāṉē collappaṭum poruḷ ciṟappac ceyvatu ēntal vaṇṇam.
ēntal eṉpatu mikutal; oru collē mikkuvarutaliṉ ēntal vaṇṇam eṉṟāyiṟṟu. atu,
''vikalum vikal varakkaṇṭu maḵtuṇarār"
eṉavarum.
nac:
ēntal vaṇṇamāvatu orukāṟ colliya collāṉē collappaṭum poruḷ ciṟappac ceytal.
ēntal oru coṉmikutal.
''vikal... tuṇarātār"
''kūṭuvār... pirivu".
eṉa varum.
u.
ā.ku:
kuṟilakaval ēntici vaṇṇam mutalāka irupatu ēntici vaṇṇaṅ kaḷik kāṭṭum yāpparuṅkala virutti nūlār, ''avitām matayāṉi naṭantāṟpōlavum pāmpu paṇittāṟ pōlavum, ōṅkip paṟakkum puṭpōlavum varumeṉak koḷka" eṉṟār. ēntal vaṇṇatti  ēntici vaṇṇameṉak koṇṭatu muttuvīriyam.


ērōr kaḷavaḻiyum tērōr kaḷavaḻiyum
a.
ērōr kaḷavaḻi aṉṟik kaḷavaḻit
tērōr tōṟṟiya veṉṟiyum	(to.po.75)
ā.	
kiṇinili
taṇpaṇivayal uḻavaṉit
teṇkiṇivaṉ tiruntupukaḻ kiḷantaṉṟu	            (pu.ve.vā.32)
kaḷavaḻi
malivayal uḻavaṉi vaṟumitīr peruvaḷam
polikeṉak kiṇivaṉ pukaḻnta kaḷavaḻiyum	(i.vi.613)
i.
iḷam:
ērōr kaḷavaḻi kūṟutalum aṉṟip pōrōr kaḷavaḻi tērōr tōṟṟuvitta veṉṟiyum.
atu kaḷampāṭutalum kaḷavēḷvi pāṭutalumām. kaḷavaḻi  kaḷattu nikaḻum ceyalkaḷ.
nac:
vēḷāṇ mākkaḷ viḷiyuṭ kālattuk kaḷattuc ceyyum ceykikaḷit tērēṟivanta  kiṇipporunar mutaliyōr pōrkkaḷattē tōṟṟuvitta veṉṟi yaṉṟik kaḷavaḻic ceykikaḷi māṟātu tērēṟi vanta pulavar tōṟṟuvitta veṉṟiyāṉum.
eṉṟatu neṟkatirik koṉṟu kaḷattiṟkuvittup pōraḻittu ataritirittuc cuṟṟattōṭu nukarvataṟku muṉṉē kaṭavuṭpali koṭuttup piṉṉar paricilāḷar mukantu koḷḷa variciyiṉ aḷikku māṟu pōla aracaṉum nāṟpaṭiyiyum koṉṟu kaḷattiṟkuvittu erutu kaḷiṟāka vāḷpaṭa ōcci atari tirittup piṇakkuviyi niṇaccēṟṟoṭu utirap pērulikkaṇ ēṟṟi īṉāvēṇmāṉ iṭantuḻantaṭṭa kūḻppaliyip paliyākak koṭuttu eñciniṉṟa yāṉi kutirikaḷiyum āṇṭup peṟṟaṉa palavaṟṟiyum paricilar mukantu koḷḷak koṭuttalām.
ī.
nāvalar:
kaḷamar kaḷattil neṟpōraṭittal mutaliya viḻavārppōṭu pōrk kaḷattil tērmaṟavar veṟṟi viḻaviṉ vīṟum.
ka.ve:
nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟum ivvuri poykiyār pāṭiya kaḷavaḻi nāṟpatu eṉṉum ilakkiyatti uḷaṅkoṇṭu eḻutappeṟṟatākum. nacciṉārk kiṉiyar karutumāṟu ittoṭar tērēṟi vanta pulavar tōṟṟuvitta veṉṟiyik kuṟittatāyiṉ atu pāṭāṇṭiṇi yākumē yaṉṟi vākit tiṇiyākātu. ākavē, 'tērōr tōṟṟiya veṉṟi' eṉpataṟkut tērēṟivanta pōrvīrarkaḷ ērkkaḷattuk kaḷamar ceyyumāṟu pōlap pōrkkaḷattut tōṟṟuvitta veṟṟic ceykikaḷ eṉap poruḷurit talē vākittiṇi yamippukku ēṟpuṭiyatākum.


ēṇimayakkam
a.
maṭiyami ēṇimici mayakkamum	(to.po.69)
ā.
ēṇiniliyē	(pu.ve.6)
toṭukaḻal maṟavar tuṉṉit tuṉṉār
iṭucūṭ ṭiñciyiṉ ēṇicāt tiṉṟu	(pu.ve.uḻiñi.18)
uṭal ciṉattār kaṭiyaraṇam
miṭal cāyamēl ivarntaṉṟu	                   (pu.ve.uḻiñi. 19)
toṭukaḻal maṟavar tuṉṉit tuṉṉār
iṭucūṭ ṭiñciyiṉ ēṇi cārttalum
āṭutal ollā araṇkā valavar
īṭaṟa ēṇiyiṉ ivareyiṟ pāciyum	(i.vi.608)
i.
iḷam:
matiliṭattu maṭuttal aminta ēṇi cārtti ataṉmēl porumpōr mayakkamum.
nac:	
mītiṭu palakiyōṭum maṭuttuc ceyyappaṭṭa ēṇimici niṉṟu puṟattōrum akattōrum pōr ceytalum.
ī.
nāvalar:
toṭiyaminta ēṇippaṭikaḷiṉ mēl ēṟuvōrum etirppōrum tammuṉ kalantu malitalum (maṭi  pūṭṭu. ēṇippakkac caṭṭaṅkaḷil paḻukkaḷ pūṭṭappaṭutalāl maṭiyami ēṇi eṉappaṭṭatu.)
u.
ā.ku:	
ēṇ ēṇi  uyaram. uyarac celvataṟku  aminta karuvi. mayaṅkutal  kalattal; ivaṇ kikalattalukku āyatu; ki kalattal karuvikalattalu mām. 'kalakam' eṉpatu aṟika.
aiñcīraṭi
a.
veṇṭaḷi viraviyum āciriyam viraviyum
aiñcīr  aṭiyum uḷaveṉa moḻipa.	(to.po.369)
i.
iḷam:
(āciriyappāviṟkuriyatōr marapuṇarttutal).
iyaṟcīr veṇṭaḷi viraviyum āciriyattaḷi viraviyum aiñcīraṭiyum āciriyappāviṉkaṇ varuvaṉa uḷa.
'veṇṭaḷi viraviyum āciriyam viraviyum' eṉṟatu eṉṉi yeṉiṉ, avviñcīraṭi veṇpāvōṭu virāyum āciriyappā vōṭu virāyum varumeṉṟavāṟu.
īṇṭut taḷi eṉṟatu ap pākkaḷiyātalāl ap pākkaḷōṭu viravumeṉavē, ap pākkaḷi uṟuppākak koṇṭu varumpiṟa ceyyuṭ kaṇṇum avviñcīraṭi varumeṉpatu peṟṟām. veṇṭaḷiyoṭu viravum eṉavē aiñcīraṭi avaṟṟukku uriyavalla veṉpatām. eṉavē, kalippāviṉ kaṇvarum aiñcīraṭiyāyiṉ akkaliyōcikku urimi yuṭitteṉpatām.
'uḷa' eṉavē aiñcīraṭi kaliyiṉuṭ payiṉṟu varumeṉpatūum allaṉa vaṟṟuṭ payilāveṉavuṅ koḷka.
'virāaya taḷiyum orūuniliyiṉṟu'	(cey. 61)
eṉappaṭṭa kalippāviṉōṭu iyipupaṭṭa veṇpā vaṭiyum ataṉōṭu iyipupaṭṭa āciriyavaṭiyum kūṟi, am mūṉṟaṉiyum uṭaṉ kūṟiṉāṉāyi ṉum āṇṭuc ciṟantavāṟē īṇṭum av vatikārattatāka nāṭṭi  aiñcīraṭi yum  uḷaveṉṟu āṇṭu niṉṟa kalikkuk kūṟiya avvaṭi veṇṭaḷi viraviyum āciriyam viraviyum varumeṉap piṟavaṟṟukkum kūṟiṉāṉ eṉpatu...
'ciṟucōṟ ṟāṉu naṉipala kalattaṉ maṉṉē
peruñcōṟ ṟāṉu naṉipala kalattaṉ maṉṉē' 	(puṟam.235)
eṉpaṉa  aiñcīrāṉ vanta āciriyavaṭi. āciriyattuḷ ivvāṟu iraṇṭaṭi oruṅku niṟkum eṉpatu aṟivittaṟku aiñcīraṭikkuc ciṟanta kalippāvōṭu vittāṉ, ataṉi muṟipiṟaḻa eṉpatu. eṉavē veṇpāviṟku aiñcīraṭi oṉṟallatu vārātāyiṟṟu. ikkaruttē paṟṟi,
''aiñcī  raṭukkalum maṇṭila mākalum
veṇpā yāppiṟ kuriya valla"
eṉṟār piṟarum eṉka.
nac:
(mūṉṟu pāviṟkum aiñcīraṭiyum varumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu).
veṇpāvōṭum kalippāvōṭum virāayum āciriyap pāvōṭu virāayum varum aiñcīraṭikaḷum uḷaveṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar.
īṇṭut taḷiyeṉṟatu ap pākkaḷi. veṇṭaḷi yeṉavē iraṇṭu veṇṭaḷiyum aṭaṅkaliṉ avaṟṟuḷ veṇcīr veṇṭaḷiyāṉ varum cīrvaki veṇpāvum cīrvakik kaliyum aṭaṅkiṉa.
iṉi, nirimutal veṇcīr (60) eṉṉuñ cūttiram itaṟkatikāra mātaliṉ kaṭṭaḷikkalikkum aiñcīraṭi koḷka. taḷiyeṉṟataṉāṟ kaṭṭaḷi veṇpāviṟkuṅ koḷka.
aṭiyum eṉṟa ummiyāṉ veṇpāviṉuḷ mikavum ciṟupāṉmi varutal koḷka.
uḷaveṉṟataṉāṟ kalippāviṉuḷḷum ataṟkuṟuppāy varum pākkaḷuḷ ḷum perumpāṉmi varutalum āciriyattuḷ aṭukki varutalum koḷka.
taṉṉiṉa muṭittal eṉpataṉāṟ kaṭṭaḷi yāciriyattiṟku  varumēṉu muṇarka. 
iṉṉum aiñcīraṭiyum eṉṟa ummiyāṟ koccakak kalikku aiñcīraṭiyum varumāṟu koḷka.
itaṉi iṉameṉpārumuḷar piṉṉuḷḷōr.
u.
ā.ku:
āciriyattuḷḷum kaliyuḷḷum aiñcīr aṭi varum eṉpatu innūṟ pāvāṟ peṟappaṭutal pōl, ''aiñcīr aṭukkalum maṇṭilam ākka lum veṇpā yāppiṟkuriya valla" eṉpataṉāl (to.po.375. mēṟ.: nakkīraṉār aṭi nūl) aiñcīr veṇpāvuḷ pukāmi aṟiyalām.


aintaṟivatu
a.
1.	aintaṟi vatuvē avaṟṟoṭu ceviyē	            (to.po.571)
2.	māvum puḷḷum aiyaṟi viṉavē
	piṟavum uḷavē akkiḷip piṟappē.	            (to.po.576)
ā.
	vāṉavar makkaḷ narakar vilaṅkupuḷ
	āti cevi aṟivō ṭiyaṟi vuyirē	(naṉ.449)
	māvum makkaḷum mēvumav vaṟivē	(i.vi.913)
i.
iḷam:
1. aiyaṟivuyirāvatu uṭampiṉāṉum vāyiṉāṉum mūkkiṉāṉum kaṇṇiṉāṉum ceviyiṉāṉum aṟivatu.
2.	nāṟkāl vilaṅkum puḷḷum aiyaṟivuṭiya; akkiḷip piṟappu piṟavum uḷa eṉṟavāṟu. piṟa āvaṉa tavaḻvaṉavaṟṟuḷ pāmpu mutalāyiṉavum nīruḷ vāḻvaṉavaṟṟuḷ  mīṉum mutaliyum āmiyum mutalāyiṉavum koḷḷappaṭum.
pērā:
1.	aintaṟivuṭiyatu avaṟṟoṭu (ūṟu, nā, mūkku, kaṇ ākiya aṟivoṭu) ceviyuṇarvuṭiyatu.
2.	(pāṭam: mākkaḷum).
aiyaṟivuṭiyaṉa vilaṅkum avipōlvaṉa orucār māṉiṭaṅkaḷu mām. akkiḷip piṟappup piṟavum uḷa.
mā eṉpaṉa nāṟkāl vilaṅku, mākkaḷ eṉappaṭuvār, maṉavuṇarcci yillātār. kiḷi eṉpaṉa, eṇkāl varuṭiyum, kuraṅkum pōlvaṉa. eṇkālavāyiṉum māveṉappaṭutaliṉ varuṭi kiḷiyāyiṟṟu. kuraṅku  nāṟkālavākaliṟ kiḷiyāyiṟṟu. piṟappeṉpaṉa kiḷiyum pāmpum mutalāyiṉa.
maṟṟup pāmpiṟkuc ceviyum kaṇṇum oṉṟēyākik kaṭcevi eṉappaṭumākaliṉ aiyaṟivilli piṟaveṉiṉ, poṟiyeṉpaṉa vaṭivu nōkkiṉa allavākaliṉ oṉṟē iraṇṭuṇarviṟkum poṟiyām eṉpatu. kiḷi eṉpatu paṟaviyākaliṉ ataṉi vēṟōtuka eṉiṉ, muṉṉiyavaṟṟiṟkum paṟaviyeṉṟōtiya tilaṉākalāṉ avvac cūttiraṅkaḷāṉē ellām aṭaṅkumeṉpatu.
ī.
civañāṉa:
āciriyar tolkāppiyar 'makkaḷ tāmē āṟaṟi vuyirē' eṉak kūṟiyirukka  makkaḷ mutaliyōri aiyaṟivuyireṉak kūṟal māṟu koḷak kūṟalām piṟaveṉiṉ, makkaḷ mutaliyōr maṉa aṟivōṭu āṟaṟivuyireṉpatu nōkkiyaṉṟē muṉṉar 'makkaḷ tēvar narakar uyartiṇi'  eṉakkūṟip pōntatūum ātaliṉ iṅṅaṉam aimpulaṉ māttiriyē nukarum aṟivuṭiya makkaḷ mutaliyōri vilaṅku mutaliyavaṟṟōṭu eṇṇi  aiyaṟivuyireṉak kūṟiṉāreṉa uyt tuṇarntu koḷka. (naṉ.459. caṅ.)


aiyakkiḷavi
a.
1.	aiyak kiḷavi āṭūuviṟ kurittē	(to.po.234)
2.	ciṟantuḻi aiyam ciṟanta teṉpa
	iḻintuḻi iḻipē cuṭṭa lāṉa.	(to.po.91)
3.	vaṇṭē iḻiyē vaḷḷi pūvē
	kaṇṇē alamaral imippē accameṉ
	ṟaṉṉavi piṟavum āṅkaṇ nikaḻa
	niṉṟavi kaḷiyum karuvi eṉpa.	(to.po.92)
ā.
maṭamā ṉōkki vaṭivum kaṇṭa
iṭamum ciṟantuḻi eytuva tiyam	(na.a.120)
eytiya iruvaruḷ ciṟantiṟi vaṉmēṟ
ṟiyuṟal eṉpa aṟivuṭiyōrē	(mā.a.8)
eḻutiya valliyum toḻilpuṉi kalaṉum
vāṭiya malarum kūṭiya vaṇṭum
naṭipayil aṭiyum puṭipeyar kaṇṇum
accamum piṟavum avaṉpāl nikaḻum
kaccamil aiyam kaṭivaṉa vākum.	(na.a.121 i.vi.490)
alamvaral kaṇṇimip paccam ukakkum
malaruyirp  pataṉil vāṭutal peyarttal
oḷirkalaṉ vaḷḷi vaṇṭuṭaṉ niḻaliṭutal
aṭinilam tōytal avaṇikaḻ aiyam
kaṭivaṉa vākum kāvalaṉ taṉakkē.	(mā.a.9)
i.
iḷam:
1.	aiyakkiḷavi talimakaṟkē urittu eṉṟavāṟu.
	talivimāṭṭu aiyakkiḷavi iṉṟu eṉṟavāṟām.. ataṉāṟ kuṟṟameṉṉiyeṉiṉ, teyvameṉṟu aiyuṟuṅkāl ataṉi muṉpu kaṇṭaṟivāḷātal vēṇṭum. kāṇāmi yiṉ aiyamilaḷ eṉka.
2.	(aiyam nikaḻum iṭam uṇarttutal).
	oruvaṉ oruttiyik kaṇṇuṟṟuḻi avviruvaruyum uyarvuṭiya rāyiṉ avviṭattu aiyam ciṟantatu eṉṟu colluvar. avar iḻipuṭiyarāyiṉ avviṭattu avaḷ iḻipiṉiyē cuṭṭi uṇartalāṉ eṉṟavāṟu.
	ciṟappu eṉpatu mikuti. aiyamikutalāvatu makkaḷuḷḷāḷ allaḷ teyvamō eṉa mēlāyiṉārōṭē aiyuṟutal. ciṟantuḻi eṉpataṟkut talimakaḷtāṉ ciṟantuḻiyum koḷḷappaṭum. avaḷik kaṇṭa iṭam aiyappaṭutaṟkuc ciṟantuḻiyum koḷḷappaṭum. uruvamikuti yuṭiya ḷātaliṉ āyattāriṭik kāṇiṉum teyvam eṉṟu aiyuṟutal. itaṉāṟ colliyatu ulakattut talimakaṉum talimakaḷumāka nammāl vēṇṭappaṭṭār. antaṇar mutalākiya nāṉku varuṇatti ṉum āyar vēṭṭuvar kuṟavar paratavar eṉṉum toṭakkattiṉum akkulat tārākiya kuṟunila maṉṉarmāṭṭum uḷarāvaraṉṟē; avarellāriṉum celvattāṉum kulattāṉum vaṭivāṉum uyarnta talimaṉum talimakaḷumāyiṉōr  māṭṭē aiyam nikaḻvatu. allātār māṭṭum avviḻi marapiṉiyē cuṭṭiyuṇarā niṟkumāta lāṉ.
	aiyappaṭuvāṉ talimakaṉ eṉṟu koḷka. talimakaḷ aiyappaṭātatu eṉṉiyeṉiṉ, avaḷ  aiyappaṭuṅkāl teyvamō eṉṟu aiyuṟal vēṇṭum. avvāṟu aiyuṟṟāl  accam varum. aḵtu ētuvākak kāma nikaḻcci yuṇṭākātu.
3.	(aiyappaṭṭāṉ tuṇitaṟkuk karuvi uṇarttutal)
	eṇṇappaṭṭa vaṇṭu mutalākiya eṭṭum piṟavumākiya avviṭattu nikaḻā niṉṟa aiyam kaḷiyum karuvi eṉṟavāṟu.
	aiyam eṉpatu atikārattāṉ vantatu. nikaḻāniṉṟavi eṉpatu kuṟuki niṉṟatu. vaṇṭāvatu mayiriṉ aṇinta pūvic cūḻum vaṇṭu. atu, payiṉṟataṉ  mēlallatu cellāmiyiṉ atuvum makkaḷuḷḷāḷ eṉṟaṟitaṟkuk karuviyāyiṟṟu. iḻi eṉpatu aṇikalaṉ. atu ceyyap paṭṭateṉat tōṟṟutalāṉum, teyvappūṇ ceyyā aṇiyātalā ṉum  atuvum aṟitaṟkuk karuviyāyiṟṟu. vaḷḷi eṉpatu muliyiṉum tōḷiṉum eḻutiya koṭi. atuvum ulakiṉ uḷḷatākit tōṉṟuta liṉ karuviyāyiṟṟu. alamaral eṉpatu taṭumāṟutal. teyvamāyiṉ niṉṟavaḻi niṟkum. avvāṟaṉṟi niṉṟuḻi niṟkiṉṟilaḷ eṉṟu cuḻaṟciyum aṟitaṟkuk karuviyāyiṟṟu. imip peṉpatu kaṇṇimit tal. teyvattiṟkuk kaṇṇimiyāmiyiṉ atuvum aṟitaṟkuk karuviyāyiṟṟu. accameṉpatu āṇmakkaḷik kaṇṭu añcutal. atu teyvattiṟku iṉmiyāṉ atuvum aṟitaṟkuk karuviyāyiṟṟu.  aṉṉavi piṟavum eṉṟataṉāṉ  kālnilan tōytal, viyarttal, niḻaliṭutal koḷka. ivi karuviyākat tuṇiyappaṭum eṉṟavāṟu.
	kāṭci mutalākiya ittuṇiyum kikkiḷik kuṟippām. iṉik kuṟippaṟital kūṟukiṉṟārākaliṉ akkuṟippu  nikaḻum vaḻi ivi yellām  akamām. eṉṉi? iruvar māṭṭum otta nikaḻcciyāta lāṉ. ivi talimakaḷ māṭṭup pulappaṭa nikaḻātu, āṇṭuk kuṟippiṉāṟ ciṟitu nikaḻum  eṉṟu koḷka.
nac:
1.	kūṟuvēmō kūṟēmō eṉṟu aiyamuṟṟuk kūṟuñcol talivaṟku urittu.
	iṉi, aiyappāṭu talivikkum urittu eṉṟāṟ  'ciṟantuḻi yiyam'  eṉpataṟku māṟām.
2.	(eytātatu eytuvittu eytiyatu vilakkiṟṟu. 'muṉṉiya nāṉkum eṉṟataṉāṟ  kūṟiya aiyam talivaṉ  kaṇṇē eṉavum  talivikku nikaḻumō eṉṉum aiyatti vilakkutaluṅ kūṟaliṉ).
	aṅṅaṉam etirppāṭṭiṉ  iruvaruḷḷum ciṟanta talivaṉ kaṇṇē  aiyam nikaḻtal ciṟantateṉṟu kūṟuvār āciriyar; attalivaṉiṉ iḻinta talivikkaṇ aiyam nikaḻumāyiṉ iṉpattiṟku iḻivē  avaḷ karutum ātalāṉ.
	talivaṟkut teyvamō allaḷō eṉa  nikaḻnta aiyam, nūṉ mutaliya vaṟṟāl nīkkit teyvamaṉmi uṇartaṟku aṟi vuṭiyaṉāta lum, talivikku murukaṉō iyakkaṉō makaṉōveṉa aiyam nikaḻiṉ ataṉi nīkki uṇartaṟkuk karuviyilaḷ  ākalāṉum iṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟiṉār. talivikku  aiyam nikaḻiṉ accamēyaṉṟik kāmak kuṟippu nikaḻātām. makaṭūuviṉ āṭūuc ciṟattal paṟṟic ciṟantuḻi eṉṟār.
3.	(iḵtu aiyuṟṟut teḷiyuṅkāl iṭiyatu ārāycciyātaliṉ ārāyum karuvi kūṟukiṉṟatu. vaṇṭu mutaliyaṉa  vāṉakattaṉa aṉṟi maṇṇakat taṉa ātal nūṟkēḷviyāṉum  uyttuṇarcciyāṉum talimakkaḷ uṇarpa).
	payiṉṟataṉ mēlallātu cellāta tātu ūtum vaṇṭu
	oruvarāl iḻikkappaṭṭa aṇikalaṉkaḷ;
	muliyiṉun tōḷiṉum eḻutum toyyiṟ koṭi;
	kikkoṇṭu mōntu uyirkkum kaḻunīrppū;
	vāṉkaṇṇallāta  ūṉkaṇ;
	kaṇṭaṟiyāta vaṭivu kaṇṭa accattāṟpiṟanta taṭumāṟṟam
	akkaṇṇiṉ itaḻ imittal;
	āṇmakaṉik kaṇṭuḻi maṉattiṟ piṟakkum accam;
	eṉṟu avveṇvakip poruḷum avipōlvaṉa piṟavum avvetirppāṭṭiṉkaṇtāṉ muṉpu kaṇṭa variyara makaḷ mutaliya piḻampukaḷāy īṇṭut taṉ maṉattu nikaḻaniṉṟa appiḻampukaḷi muntu nūṟkaṇṇē avviyam nīkkum karuviyāmeṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar. eṉavē, eṉakkum atu karutteṉṟār. iviyellām makkaṭ kuriyaṉavāy nikaḻavē teyvap pakutimēṟ ceṉṟa aiyam nīṅkit tuṇiyum uḷḷam  piṟattaliṉ tuṇivum uṭaṉkūṟiṟṟē āyiṟṟu. 'aṉṉavāvaṉa  kālnilam tōytalum, niḻaliṭum viyarttalum mutaliyaṉa.
	iṅṅaṉam aiyam tīrntuḻit taliviyi viyantu kūṟutaluṅ koḷka.
ī.
ka.ve:
2. talivi  ottanalaṅkaḷāṟ ciṟantu tōṉṟiyavaḻi (talivaṉatu uḷḷattē)  aiyantōṉṟutal ciṟantatu eṉpar. ciṟappiṉṟit tāḻnta niliyil avviḻipē iṉṉa taṉmiyaḷ eṉat teḷivikkumātaliṉ (inniliyil aiyam tōṉṟutaṟku iṭamilli).
aiyakkiḷaviyāvatu ivar makkaḷiṉattārō aṉṟit teyvamō eṉat tammiṉum mēlāyiṉārōṭu vittu eṇṇi aiyuṟṟuk kūṟuñcol.



aiyakkiḷaviyiṉ aṟital
a.
kiḻavōṉ aṟiyā aṟiviṉaḷ ivaḷeṉa
miyaṟu ciṟappiṉ uyarntōr pāṅkiṉ
aiyak kiḷaviyiṉ aṟitalum urittē.	(to.po.115)
i.
iḷam:
(naṟṟāyum ceviliyum tuṇiyumāṟu).
talivaṉ aṟiyā aṟiviṉiyuṭiyavaḷ eṉak kuṟṟamaṟṟa ciṟappiṉi yuṭiya uyarntōr māṭṭu uḷatākiya aiyak kiḷaviyāl puṇarppaṟitalum urittu cevilikkum  naṟṟāykkum eṉṟavāṟu.
kiḻavōṉ aṟiyā aṟiviṉaḷ eṉpatu talimakaṉaṟiyā aṟiviṉi yuṭiyavaḷ eṉṟavāṟu. eṉavē, orupakkam etirkālam nōkkik kūṟiṉār pōlat tōṉṟum; orupakkam iṟantakālam tōṉṟum. avaṉ aṟiyāta aṟivurimi pūṇṭu mayaṅkutal. avaḷ ettuṇiyum mayakkamilaḷ eṉavum, avaṉ poruṭṭu mayaṅkiṉāḷ eṉavum paṭakkūṟutal.
iḵtu eṟṟiṉāṉ āyiṟṟu eṉak kuṟṟamaṟṟa tamari viṉāyavaḻi avar ivvāṟupaṭṭateṉa meykūṟutalum takutiyaṉṟām. poy kūṟutalum takutiyaṉṟām; ātalāl aiyappaṭumāṟu kūṟiyavaḻi ataṉāṉē yuṇarpa eṉṟavāṟu.
kiḻavōṉ aṟiyā aṟiviṉaḷ eṉṟavāṟu kūṟiyavaḻik kiḻavaṉ etirppaṭa iṟanta kālattuḷ talivaṉ uḷaṉ eṉṟavāṟām.
nac:
(iḵtu  aṅṅaṉaṅ kaḷavu veḷippaṭṭa piṉṉar naṟṟāykkuñ cevilikkum uriyatōr  ilakkaṇam kūṟukiṉṟatu).
naṅkulattiṟku otta talivaṉi aṟintu kūṭāta aṟiviṉi yuṭiyaḷ ivaḷeṉṟu tam maṉattē aiyamuṟṟum piṟarōṭum ucāvum kiḷaviyik kuṟṟamaṟṟa ciṟappiṉi yuṭiya antaṇar mutaliyō riṭattē kūṟi atuvum   muṟimiyeṉṟu avar kūṟa aṟitalum urittu.
eṉṟatu, 'mikkōṉāyiṉum kaṭivariyiṉṟē' eṉa muṟkūṟiṉamiyiṉ talivaṉ taṉ kulattiṉ uyarntami aṟintaviṭattu iṅṅaṉaṅ kūṭutal muṟiyaṉṟeṉṟu aiyuṟṟa ceviliyum naṟṟāyum uyarntōrik kēṭṭu ituvuṅ kūṭumuṟimi yeṉṟuṇarvar eṉpatām.  ilakkaṇamuṇmi yiṉ ilakkiyamum akkālattuḷa veṉṟuṇarka.
ī.
ka.ve:
(naṟṟāyum ceviliyum talimakaḷatu oḻukalāṟṟi aiyuṟṟut tuṇiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu).
'talimakaṉ aṟiyā aṟiviṉiyuṭiyāḷ ivaḷ' eṉṟu kuṟṟam aṟutta ciṟappiṉiyuṭiya uṇarntōr pakkattu uḷatākiya aiyakkiḷaviyāl talivaṉōṭu talivik kuḷatākiya puṇarppiṉi aṟintu koḷḷutal cevilikkum naṟṟāykkum uriyatu.
taliviyiṉ meliviṉik kaṇṭu varuntiya ceviliyum naṟṟāyum kuṟṟamaṟṟa ciṟappiṉiyuṭiya uyarntōrākiya aṟivarip paṇintu tam makaḷatu melivu etaṉālāyiṟṟu eṉa  viṉavi niṟpar.  anniliyil  mukkāla nikaḻccikaḷiyum oruṅkuṇarum nuṇṇuṇarvuṭiya  ap periyōrkaḷ 'ivaḷ, ittaṉmiyāṉ oruvaṉik kātalikkiṉ ṟāḷ'  eṉat taliviyōṭu talivaṉuk kuṇṭākiya toṭarpiṉi veḷippaṭac collutal marapaṉmiyāṉum, nikaḻntataṉi maṟittal tamatu vāymikku māṟātalāṉum 'nummakaḷ talimakaṉaṟiyā aṟiviṉi yuṭiyāḷ' eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟu aiyuṟutaṟkuriya kavartta poruḷuṭiya toṭarāl maṟumoḻi kūṟuvar.  etirkālattil taṉṉi maṇantu koḷḷum urimiyuṭiya kaṇavaṉālum aṟiyappaṭāta pēraṟi viṉi yuṭiyāḷ num makaḷākiya ivaḷ' eṉa veḷip paṭiyāka oru poruḷum, 'taṉ kātaṟkiḻami yuṭiyavaṉāka oḻukum talimakaṉālum  aṟiyap paṭāta aṟivurimi pūṇṭu avaṉ poruṭṭu mayaṅkukiṉṟāḷ ivaḷ' eṉak kuṟippāka maṟṟoru poruḷum tarum niliyil  aṟivar kūṟiya aiyakkiḷaviyi uyt tuṇarntu talivaṉ oruvaṉuṭaṉ tam makaḷukku uḷatākiya toṭarpiṉic ceviliyum naṟṟāyum aṟintu koḷḷutaṟkum uriyar eṉpatu  innūṟpāviṉāl aṟivuṟuttappaṭum nuṇporuḷākum.


aiyaṅ kaḷiyuṅ karuvi
a.
vaṇṭē iḻiyē vaḷḷi pūvē
kaṇṇē alamaral imippē accameṉ
ṟaṉṉavi piṟavum āṅkaṇ nikaḻa
niṉṟavi kaḷiyuṅ karuvi eṉpa.	(to.po.92)
ā.	
eḻutiya valliyum toḻilpuṉi kalaṉum
vāṭiya malarum kūṭiya vaṇṭum
naṭipayil aṭiyum puṭipeyar kaṇṇum
accamum piṟavum avaṉpāl nikaḻum
kaccamil aiyaṅ kaṭivaṉa vākum.	(na.a.121) (i.vi.490)
alamvaral kaṇṇimip paccam ukakkum
malaruyirp pataṉil vāṭutal veyarttal
oḷirkalaṉ vaḷḷi vaṇṭuṭaṉ niḻaliṭutal
aṭinilam tōytal avaṉikaḻ aiyam
kaṭivaṉa ākum kāvalaṉ taṉakkē.	(mā.a.9)
i.
iḷam:
1.	aiyakkiḷavi talimakaṟkē urittu eṉṟavāṟu.	talivimāṭṭu aiyak kiḷavi iṉṟu eṉṟavāṟām.  ataṉāṟ kuṟṟameṉṉiyeṉiṉ, teyvameṉṟu aiyuṟuṅkāl ataṉi muṉpu kaṇṭaṟivāḷātal vēṇṭum.  kāṇāmiyiṉ aiyamilaḷ eṉka.
2.	(aiyam nikaḻum iṭam uṇarttutal)
	oruvaṉ oruttiyik kaṇṇuṟṟuḻi avviru varum  uyarvuṭiya rāyiṉ avviṭattu aiyam ciṟantatu eṉṟu colluvar. avar iḻipuṭiyarāyiṉ avviṭattu avaḷ iḻipiṉiyē cuṭṭi uṇartalāṉ eṉṟavāṟu.
	ciṟappu eṉpatu mikuti. aiyamikutalāvatu makkaḷuḷḷāḷ allaḷ teyvamō eṉa mēlāyiṉārōṭē aiyuṟutal.  ciṟantuḻi eṉpataṟkut  talimakaḷtāṉ ciṟantuḻiyum koḷḷappaṭum. avaḷik kaṇṭa iṭam aiyappaṭutaṟkuc ciṟantuḻiyum koḷḷappaṭum. uruvamikuti yuṭiya ḷātaliṉ āyattāriṭik kāṇiṉum teyvam eṉṟu aiyuṟutal. itaṉāṟ colliyatu ulakattut talimakaṉum talimakaḷumāka nammāl vēṇṭappaṭṭār, antaṇar mutalākiya nāṉku varuṇatti ṉum āyar vēṭṭuvar kuṟavar paratavar eṉṉum toṭakkattiṉum akkulattārākiya kuṟunila maṉṉar māṭṭum uḷarāvaraṉṟē; avarellāriṉum celvattāṉum kulattāṉum vaṭivāṉum uyarnta talimaṉum talimakaḷumāyiṉōr  māṭṭē aiyam nikaḻvatu. allātār māṭṭum avviḻi marapiṉiyē cuṭṭiyuṇarā niṟku mātalāṉ.
	aiyappaṭuvāṉ talimakaṉ eṉṟu koḷka. talimakaḷ aiyappaṭātatu eṉṉiyeṉiṉ, avaḷ aiyappaṭuṅkāl  teyvamō eṉṟu aiyuṟal vēṇṭum.  avvāṟu  aiyuṟṟāl accam varum. aḵtu ētuvākak kāma nikaḻcci yuṇṭākātu.
3.	(aiyappaṭṭāṉ tuṇitaṟkuk karuvi uṇarttutal).
	eṇṇappaṭṭa vaṇṭu mutalākiya eṭṭum piṟavumākiya avviṭattu nikaḻā niṉṟa aiyaṅkaḷiyuṅ karuvi eṉṟavāṟu.
	aiyam  eṉpatu  atikārattāṉ vantatu. nikaḻāniṉṟavi eṉpatu kuṟuki niṉṟatu. vaṇṭāvatu mayiriṉ aṇinta pūvic cūḻum vaṇṭu. atu, payiṉṟataṉ mēlallatu cellāmiyiṉ  atuvum makkaḷuḷḷāḷ eṉṟaṟitaṟkuk  karuviyāyiṟṟu.  iḻi eṉpatu aṇikalaṉ. atu ceyyappaṭṭateṉat tōṟṟutalāṉum, teyvappūṇ ceyyā  aṇiyātalāṉum atuvum aṟitaṟkuk karuviyāyiṟṟu.  vaḷḷi eṉpatu muliyilum tōḷilum  eḻutiya koṭi.  atuvum ulakiṉ uḷḷa tākit tōṉṟutaliṉ karuviyā yiṟṟu.  alamaral eṉpatu taṭumāṟutal, teyvamāyiṉ niṉṟavaḻi niṟkum; avvāṟaṉṟi niṉṟuḻi niṟkiṉṟilaḷ eṉṟu cuḻaṟciyum  aṟitaṟkuk karuviyāyiṟṟu.  imip peṉpatu kaṇṇimittal. teyvattiṟkuk kaṇṇimi yāmiyiṉ atuvum aṟitaṟkuk karuviyāyiṟṟu.  accameṉpatu āṇmakkaḷik kaṇṭu añcutal. atu teyvattiṟku  iṉmiyāṉ atuvum aṟitaṟkuk karuviyāyiṟṟu.  aṉṉavi piṟavum eṉṟataṉāṉ kālnilantōytal, viyarttal, niḻaliṭutal koḷka. ivi karuviyākat tuṇiyappaṭum eṉṟavāṟu.
	kāṭci mutalākiya ittuṇiyum kikkiḷik kuṟippām. iṉik kuṟippaṟital kūṟukiṉṟārākaliṉ ak kuṟippu nikaḻum vaḻi ivi yellām akamām. eṉṉi? iruvar māṭṭum otta nikaḻcciyāta lāṉ. ivi talimakaḷ māṭṭup pulappaṭa nikaḻātu. āṇṭuk kuṟippiṉāṟ ciṟitu nikaḻum eṉṟu koḷka.
nac:	
1. 	kūṟuvēmō kūṟēmō eṉṟu aiyamuṟṟuk kūṟuñcol talivaṟku urittu.
	iṉi, aiyappāṭu talivikkum urittu eṉṟāṟ 'ciṟantuḻi yiyam' eṉpataṟku māṟām.
2.	(eytātatu eytuvittu eytiyatu vilakkiṟṟu. 'muṉṉiya nāṉkum eṉṟataṉāṟ kūṟiya aiyam talivaṉ kaṇṇatē eṉavum talivikku nikaḻumō eṉṉum  aiyatti vilakkutalum kūṟaliṉ).
	aṅṅaṉam etirppāṭṭiṉ iruvaruḷḷum ciṟanta talivaṉ kaṇṇē  aiyam nikaḻtal ciṟantateṉṟu kūṟuvār āciriyar; attalivaṉiṉ iḻinta talivikkaṇ aiyam nikaḻumāyiṉ iṉpattiṟku iḻivē avaḷ karutum ātalāṉ.
	talivaṟkut teyvamō allaḷōeṉa nikaḻnta aiyam, nūl mutaliya vaṟṟāl  nīkkit teyvamaṉmi uṇartaṟku aṟivuṭiyaṉāka lāṉum, talivikku murukaṉō iyakkaṉō makaṉōveṉa aiyam nikaḻiṉ ataṉi nīkki uṇartaṟkuk karuviyilaḷ ākalāṉum  iṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟiṉār. talivikku aiyam nikaḻiṉ accamēyaṉṟik kāmak kuṟippu nikaḻātām. makaṭūuviṉ āṭūuc ciṟattal paṟṟic ciṟantuḻi eṉṟār. 
3.	(iḵtu aiyuṟṟut teḷiyuṅkāl iṭiyatu ārāycci yātaliṉ ārāyum karuvi kūṟukiṉṟatu. vaṇṭu mutaliyaṉa vāṉakattaṉa aṉṟi maṇṇakat taṉa ātal nūṟkēḷviyā ṉum uyttuṇarcciyāṉum talimakkaḷ uṇarpa).
	payiṉṟataṉ mēlallātu cellāta tātu ūtum vaṇṭu;
	oruvarāl iḻikkappaṭṭa aṇikalaṉkaḷ;
	muliyiṉun  tōḻiṉum eḻutum toyyiṟ koṭi;
	kikkoṇṭu mōntu uyirkkum kaḻunīrppū;
	vāṉkaṇṇillāta ūṇkaṇ;
	kaṇṭaṟiyāta vaṭivu kaṇṭa  accattāṟpiṟanta taṭumāṟṟam;
	akkaṇṇiṉ itaḻ imittal;
	āṇmakaṉik kaṇṭuḻi maṉattiṟpiṟakkum accam; 
	eṉṟu avveṇvakip poruḷum avipōlvaṉa piṟavum avvetirppāṭṭiṉkaṇ muṉpu  kaṇṭa variyara makaḷ mutaliya piḻampukaḷāy īṇṭut taṉ  maṉattu nikaḻa niṉṟa appiḻampukaḷi  muntu nūṟkaṇṇē avviyam nīkkum karuviyāmeṉṟu  kūṟuvar āciriyar.  eṉavē, eṉakkum atu karutteṉṟār.  iviyellām makkaṭ kuriyaṉavāy nikaḻavē teyvappakutimēṟ ceṉṟa aiyam nīṅkat tuṇiyum uḷḷam piṟattaliṉ tuṇivum uṭaṉkūṟiṟṟē āyiṟṟu.
	iṅṅaṉam aiyam tīrntuḻit taliviyi viyantu kūṟutaluṅ koḷka.
ī.
ka.ve:
2. talivi ottanalaṅkaḷāṟ ciṟantu tōṉṟiyavaḻi (talivaṉatu uḷḷattē) aiyantōṉṟutal  ciṟantatu eṉpar.  ciṟappiṉṟit tāḻnta niliyil avviḻipē iṉṉa taṉmiyaḷ eṉat teḷivikkumātaliṉ (inniliyil aiyam tōṉṟutaṟku iṭamilli).
	aiyakkiḷaviyāvatu ivar makkaḷiṉattārō aṉṟit teyvamō eṉat tammiṉum mēlāyiṉārōṭu vittu eṇṇi aiyuṟṟuk kūṟuñcol.
nac:
